(*These are the PDE's for pde_in_cas web page in Mathematica syntax.*) 
(*Collected by Nasser M. Abbasi. These are the PDE used to make the webpage at *)
(*https://www.12000.org/my_notes/pde_in_CAS/pde_in_cas.htm*)
(*Any corrections, please report to nma@12000.org*)
(*This file was last updated on Sun 19 Jul 2026 04:03:08


(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1*)


pde =  D[u[x, t], {t}] + D[u[x, t], {x}] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, u[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 2*)


pde =  D[u[t, x], {t}] -3* D[u[t, x], {x}] == 0;
ic = u[0,x]==Exp[-x^2];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde,ic}, u[t, x], {t, x}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 3*)


pde =  D[u[t, x], {t}] +2* D[u[t, x], {x}] == 0;
ic = u[-1,x]==x/(1+x^2);
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde,ic}, u[t, x], {t, x}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 4*)


pde =  D[u[t, x], {t}] + D[u[t, x], {x}]+1/2*u[t,x] == 0;
ic = u[0,x]==ArcTan[x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde,ic}, u[t, x], {t, x}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 5*)


pde =  D[u[t, x], {t}] -4*D[u[t, x], {x}]+u[t,x] == 0;
ic = u[0,x]==1/(1+x^2);
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde,ic}, u[t, x], {t, x}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 6*)


pde =  D[u[t, x], {t}] +2*D[u[t, x], {x}]== Sin[x];
ic = u[0,x]==Sin[x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde,ic}, u[t, x], {t, x}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 7*)


pde =  D[u[x,t],t] +1/(1+x^2)*D[u[x,t],x]== 0;
ic = u[x,0]==1/(1+(3+x)^2);
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde,ic}, u[x,t], {x,t}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 8*)


pde =  D[u[x,t], t] -x*D[u[x,t], x]== 0;
ic = u[x,0]==1/(1+x^2);
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde,ic}, u[x,t], {x,t}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 9*)


pde =  D[u[x,t], t] +(1-2*t)*D[u[x,t], x]== 0;
ic = u[x,0]==1/(1+x^2);
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde,ic}, u[x,t], {x,t}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 10*)


pde =  D[u[x,t], t] +1/(x^2+4)*D[u[x,t], x]== 0;
ic = u[x,0]==Exp[x^3+12*x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde,ic}, u[x,t], {x,t}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 11*)


sol = AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[3*D[u[x, y], x] + 5*D[u[x, y], y] == x, u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 12*)

pde = x*D[u[x, y], y] + y*D[u[x, y], x] == -4*x*y*u[x, y];
ic  = u[x, 0] == Exp[-x^2];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, ic}, u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 13*)


pde =  D[u[x, t], t] + D[u[x, t], x] == 0;
bc  = u[0, t] == 0;
ic  = u[x, 0] == Sin[x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, ic, bc}, u[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 14*)

ic  = u[x, 0] == Exp[-x^2];
pde =  D[u[x, t], {t}] + c*D[u[x, t], {x}] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, ic}, u[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 15*)

pde = D[w[x, t], t] - 3*D[w[x, t], x] == 0;
ic  = w[x, 0] == Cos[x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, ic}, w[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 16*)


pde =  D[w[x, t], t] + c*D[w[x, t], x] == 0;
ic  = w[x, 0] == f[x];
bc  = w[0, t] == h[t];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, ic, bc}, w[x, t], {x, t}, Assumptions -> c > 0 && x > 0 && t > 0], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 17*)

pde = D[w[x, t], t] + c*D[w[x, t], x] == Exp[2*x];
ic  = w[x, 0] == f[x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[Simplify[DSolve[{pde, ic}, w[x, t], {x, t}, Assumptions -> c > 0 && x > 0 && t > 0]], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 18*)

pde = D[w[x, t], t] + 3*t*D[w[x, t], x] == w[x, t];
ic  = w[x, 0] == f[x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[Simplify[DSolve[{pde, ic}, w[x, t], {x, t}]], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 19*)


pde =  2*D[u[x, y], x] + 5*D[u[x, y], y] == u[x, y]^2 + 1;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[Simplify[DSolve[pde, u[x, y], {x, y}]], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 20*)

pde = u[x, y] == x*D[u[x, y], {x}] + y*D[u[x, y], {y}] + (1/2)*(D[u[x, y], {x}]^2 + D[u[x, y], {y}]^2);
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[Simplify[DSolve[pde, u[x, y], {x, y}]], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 21*)


pde =  u[x, y] == x*D[u[x, y], {x}] + y*D[u[x, y], {y}] + (1/2)*(D[u[x, y], {x}]^2 + D[u[x, y], {y}]^2);
ic  = u[x, 0] == (1*(1 - x^2))/2;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, ic}, u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 22*)

pde = u[x, y] == x*D[u[x, y], x] + y*D[u[x, y], y] + Sin[D[u[x, y], x] + D[u[x, y], y]];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 23*)


 eq1 = D[f[x, y], x] == x*y*Cos[x*y] + Sin[x*y];
 eq2 = D[f[x, y], y] == -E^(-y) + x^2*Cos[x*y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{eq1, eq2}, f[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 24*)

pde = x*D[f[x, y], y] - D[f[x, y], x] == (f[x, y]^2*g[x])/h[y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, f[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 25*)


pde =  D[f[x, y, z], x] + D[f[x, y, z], y]^2 == f[x, y, z] + z;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, f[x, y, z], {x, y, z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 26*)


pde = x*D[u[x, y], x] + y*D[u[x, y], y] ==u[x, y];
sol = AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 27*)


pde =  x*D[u[x, y], x] + y*D[u[x, y], y] ==n*u[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 28*)


pde =  x^2*D[u[x, y], x] + y^2*D[u[x, y], y] ==(x+y)*u[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 29*)


pde =  (y - z)*D[u[x, y, z], x] + (z - x)*D[u[x, y, z], y] + (x - y)*D[u[x, y, z], z] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, u[x, y, z], {x, y, z}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 30*)


pde =  u[x,y]*(x+y)*D[u[x, y], x] + u[x,y]*(x-y)*D[u[x, y], y] ==x^2+y^2;
ic  = u[x,2*x]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde,ic} ,u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 31*)


pde =  D[u[x, y], x] - D[u[x, y], y] ==1;
ic  = u[x,0]==x^2;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde,ic} ,u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 32*)


pde =  y*D[u[x, y], x] +x*D[u[x, y], y] ==u[x,y];
ic  = {u[x,0]==x^3,u[0,y]==y^3};
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde,ic} ,u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 33*)


pde =  x*D[u[x, y], x] +y*D[u[x, y], y] ==x*Exp[-u[x,y]];
ic  = u[x,x^2]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde,ic}, u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 34*)


pde =  D[u[x, t], t] +u[x,t]*D[u[x, t], x] ==x;
ic  = u[x,0]==f[x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde,ic}, u[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 35*)


pde =  D[u[x, y], x] ==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde ,u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 36*)


pde =  a*D[u[x, y], x] + b*D[u[x, y], y] ==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde ,u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 37*)


pde =  D[u[x, y], x] + y*D[u[x, y], y] ==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde ,u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 38*)


pde =  (1+x^2)*D[u[x, y], x] + D[u[x, y], y] ==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde ,u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 39*)


pde =  2*x*y*D[u[x, y], x] + (x^2+y^2)*D[u[x, y], y] ==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde ,u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 40*)


pde =  (y+u[x,y])*D[u[x, y], x] + y*D[u[x, y], y] ==x-y;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde ,u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 41*)


pde =  y^2*D[u[x, y], x] - x*y*D[u[x, y], y] ==x*(u[x,y]-2*y);
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde ,u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 42*)


pde =  y*D[u[x, y], y] - x*D[u[x, y], x] ==1;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde ,u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 43*)


pde =  D[u[x, y], x] + 2*x*y^2*D[u[x,y],y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde ,u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 44*)


pde =  3*D[u[x, y], x] + 2*D[u[x,y],y] == 0;
ic  = u[x,0]==Sin[x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde,ic} ,u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 45*)


pde =  y*D[u[x, y], x] + x*D[u[x,y],y] == 0;
ic  = u[0,y]==Exp[-y^2];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde,ic} ,u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 46*)


pde =  x*D[u[x, y], x] + y*D[u[x,y],y] == 0;
ic  = u[x,x^2]==2;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde,ic} ,u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 47*)


pde =  D[u[x, y], x] + x*D[u[x,y],y] == 0;
ic  = u[0,y]==Sin[y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde,ic} ,u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 48*)


pde =  y*D[u[x, y], x] + x*D[u[x,y],y] == x*y;
ic  = {u[0,y]==Exp[-y^2],u[x,0]==Exp[-x^2]};
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde,ic} ,u[x, y], {x, y},Assumptions->{x>0,y>0}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 49*)


pde =  D[u[x, y], x] + x*D[u[x,y],y] == (y-x^2/2)^2;
ic  = u[0,y]==Exp[y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde,ic} ,u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 50*)


pde =  x*D[u[x, y], x] + y*D[u[x,y],y] == u[x,y]+1;
ic  = u[x,x^2]==x^2;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde,ic} ,u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 51*)


pde =  u[x,y]*D[u[x, y], x] - u[x,y]*D[u[x, y], y]== u[x,y]^2+ (x+y)^2;
ic  = u[x,0]==1;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde,ic} ,u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 52*)


pde =  x*D[u[x, y], x] +(x+y)*D[u[x, y], y]== u[x,y]+1;
ic  = u[x,0]==x^2;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde,ic} ,u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 53*)


pde =  x*D[u[x, y,z], x] +y*D[u[x, y,z], y]+z*D[u[x, y,z], z]== 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde ,u[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 54*)


pde =  x^2*D[u[x, y,z], x] +y^2*D[u[x, y,z], y]+z*(x+y)*D[u[x, y,z], z]== 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde ,u[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 55*)


pde =  x*(y-z)*D[u[x, y,z], x] +y*(z-x)*D[u[x, y,z], y]+z*(x-y)*D[u[x, y,z], z]== 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde ,u[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 56*)


pde =  y*z*D[u[x, y,z], x] - x*z*D[u[x, y,z], y]+x*y*(x^2+y^2)*D[u[x, y,z], z]== 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde ,u[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 57*)


pde =  x*(y^2-z^2)*D[u[x, y,z], x] +y*(z^2-y^2)*D[u[x, y,z], y]+z*(x^2-y^2)*D[u[x, y,z], z]== 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde ,u[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 58*)


pde =  D[u[x, y], x] +x*D[u[x, y], y]== y;
ic  = u[0,y]==y^2;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde,ic} ,u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 59*)


pde =  D[u[x, y], x] +x*D[u[x, y], y]== y;
ic  = u[1,y]==2*y;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde,ic} ,u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 60*)


pde =  (D[u[x, y], x] +D[u[x, y], y])^2-u[x,y]^2== 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde ,u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 61*)


pde =  (y+u[x,y])*D[u[x, y], x] +y*D[u[x, y], y]== x-y;
 ic=u[x,1]==1+x;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde,ic} ,u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 62*)


pde =  2*x*D[u[x, y], x] +(x+1)*D[u[x, y], y]== y;
 ic=u[1,y]==2*y;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde,ic} ,u[x, y], {x, y}, Assumptions->x>0], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 63*)


pde =  x*D[u[x, y], x] +y*D[u[x, y], y]== x^2+y^2;
 ic=u[x,1]==x^2;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde,ic} ,u[x, y], {x, y},Assumptions->{x>0,y>0}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 64*)


pde =  y^2*D[u[x, y], x] +(x*y)*D[u[x, y], y]== x;
 ic=u[x,1]==x^2;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde,ic} ,u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 65*)


pde =  x*D[u[x, y], x] +y*D[u[x, y], y]== x*y;
 ic=u[x,x]==x^2/2;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde ,u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 66*)


pde =  D[u[x, y], x] +u[x,y]*D[u[x, y], y]== 1;
 ic=u[0,y]==a*y;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde,ic} ,u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 67*)


pde =  (y+u[x,y])*D[u[x, y], x] +(x+u[x,y])*D[u[x, y], y]== x+y;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde ,u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 68*)


pde =  x*u[x,y]*(u[x,y]^2+x*y)*D[u[x, y], x] -y*u[x,y]*(u[x,y]^2+x*y)*D[u[x, y], y]== x^4;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde ,u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 69*)


pde =  (x+y)*D[u[x, y], x] +(x-y)*D[u[x, y], y]== 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde ,u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 70*)


pde = y*D[u[x, y], x] - x*D[u[x, y], y] == Exp[u[x, y]];
sol = AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, u[0, y] == y^2 - 1}, u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 71*)


pde = y*D[u[x, y], x] - x*D[u[x, y], y] == Exp[u[x, y]];
sol = AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 72*)


pde = D[u[x, t], t] + x*D[u[x, t], x] == 0;
ic  = u[x,0]==x^2;
sol = AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde,ic}, u[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 73*)


pde = D[u[x, t], t] + t*D[u[x, t], x] == 0;
ic  = u[x,0]==Exp[x];
sol = AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde,ic}, u[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 74*)


pde = 2 D[u[x, y], x] + 3*D[u[x, y], y] == 1;
sol = AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 75*)


pde = x D[u[x, t], t] - t*D[u[x, t], x] == 0;
sol = AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, u[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 76*)


pde = D[u[x, t], t] + D[u[x, t], x] == 0;
ic  = u[x,1]== x/(1+1+x^2);
sol = AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde,ic}, u[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 77*)


pde = D[u[x, y], x] * D[u[x, y], y] == 1;
sol = AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 78*)


pde = D[u[x, y], x] * D[u[x, y], y] == u[x,y];
bc={u[x,0]==0,u[0,y]==0};
sol = AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, bc},u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 79*)


pde = D[u[x, t], {x,2}] + D[D[u[x,t],x],t] -  6*D[u[x, t], {t,2}] == 0;
ic  = {u[x,0]==x, Derivative[0,1][u][x,0]==0};
sol = AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde,ic}, u[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 80*)


pde = D[u[x, t], {x,2}] - D[D[u[x,t],x],t] -  12*D[u[x, t], {t,2}] == 0;
ic  = {u[x,0]==0, Derivative[0,1][u][x,0]==x};
sol = AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde,ic}, u[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 81*)


pde = D[u[x, t], {x,2}] - 3*D[D[u[x,t],x],t] -  4*D[u[x, t], {t,2}] == 0;
ic  = {u[x,0]==Exp[x], Derivative[0,1][u][x,0]==0};
sol = AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde,ic}, u[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 82*)


pde = D[u[x, t], {t,2}] - 2*D[D[u[x,t],x],t] -  3*D[u[x, t], {x,2}] == 0;
ic  = {u[x,0]==x^2, Derivative[0,1][u][x,0]==Exp[x]};
sol = AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde,ic}, u[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 83*)


pde =  I*h*D[f[x, t], t] == -((h^2*D[f[x, t], {x, 2}])/(2*m));
bc  = {f[0, t] == 0, f[L, t] == 0};
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, bc}, f[x, t], {x, t}, Assumptions -> L > 0], 60*10]];
sol =  sol /. K[1] -> n;
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 84*)


pde =  I*D[f[x, y, t], {t}] == -((hBar^2*Laplacian[f[x, y, t], {x, y}])/(2*m));
 initSum = f[x, y, 0] == Sqrt[2]*(Sin[2*Pi*x]*Sin[Pi*y] + Sin[Pi*x]*Sin[2*Pi*y]);
 bcs = {f[0, y, t] == 0, f[1, y, t] == 0, f[x, 1, t] == 0, f[x, 0, t] == 0};
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, bcs, initSum}, f[x, y, t], {x, y, t}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 85*)


pde =  I*D[f[x, t], t] == -2*D[f[x, t], {x, 2}];
 g[x_] := -350 + 155*x - 22*x^2 + x^3;
ic  = f[x, 2] == g[x];
bc  = {f[5, t] == 0, f[10, t] == 0};
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, bc, ic}, f[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];
sol =  sol /. K[1] -> n;
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 86*)


pde =  I*D[f[x, t], t] == -D[f[x, t], {x, 2}] + 2*x^2*f[x, t];
bc  = {f[-Infinity, t] == 0, f[Infinity, t] == 0};
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, bc}, f[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];
sol =  sol /. K[1] -> n;
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 87*)


ic  = Piecewise[{{A*x*(a - x), 0 <= x <= a}, {0, True}}];
pde =  I*h*D[f[x, t], t] == -((h^2*D[f[x, t], {x, 2}])/(2*m));
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, f[x, 0] == ic}, f[x, t], {x, t}, Assumptions -> a > 0], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 88*)


ic  = f[x, y, 0] == Sqrt[2]*(Sin[2*Pi*x]*Sin[Pi*y] + Sin[Pi*x]*Sin[3*Pi*y]);
bc  = {f[0, y, t] == 0, f[1, y, t] == 0, f[x, 1, t] == 0, f[x, 0, t] == 0};
pde =  I*h*D[f[x, y, t], t] == -((h^2*(D[f[x, y, t], {x, 2}] + D[f[x, y, t], {y, 2}]))/(2*m));
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, ic, bc}, f[x, y, t], {x, y, t}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 89*)


ic  = f[x, y, 0] == Sqrt[2]*(Sin[2*Pi*x]*Sin[Pi*y]+Sin[Pi*x]*Sin[3*Pi*y]);
bc  = {f[0, y, t] == 0, f[1, y, t] == 0, f[x, 1, t] == 0, f[x, 0, t] == 0};
pde =  I*h*D[f[x, y,t], t] == -h^2/(2*m)*(D[f[x, y, t], {x, 2}]+D[f[x, y, t], {y, 2}]);
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, bc, ic}, f[x, y, t], {x, y, t}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 90*)


pde =  D[u[x, t], {t, 2}] + D[u[x, t], {x, 4}] == 0;
bc  = {u[0, t] == -12*t^2, u[1, t] == 1 - 12*t^2, Derivative[2, 0][u][0, t] == 0, Derivative[2, 0][u][1, t] == 12};
ic  = {u[x, 0] == x^4, Derivative[0, 1][u][x, 0] == 0};
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, ic, bc}, u[x, t], x, t], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 91*)


pde =  D[u[x, y], {x}] + u[x, y]*D[u[x, y], {y}] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[Simplify[DSolve[pde, u[x, y], {x, y}]], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 92*)


pde =  D[u[x, y], {x}] + u[x, y]*D[u[x, y], {y}] == 0;
ic  = u[x, 0] == 1/(x + 1);
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[Simplify[DSolve[{pde, ic}, u[x, y], {x, y}]], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 93*)


pde =  D[u[x, t], {t}] + u[x, t]*D[u[x, t], {x}] == \[Mu]*D[u[x, t], {x, 2}];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, u[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 94*)


pde =  D[u[x, t], {t}] + u[x, t]*D[u[x, t], {x}] == mu*D[u[x, t], {x, 2}];
ic  = u[x, 0] == Piecewise[{{1, x < 0}, {0, x >= 1}}];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, ic}, u[x, t], {x, t}, Assumptions -> mu > 0], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 95*)


pde =  D[u[x, t], {t}] + u[x, t]*D[u[x, t], {x}] == mu*D[u[x, t], {x, 2}];
ic  = u[x, 0] == UnitBox[x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, ic}, u[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 96*)


pde =  D[u[x, t], t] == (1*sigma^2*D[u[x, t], {x, 2}])/2;
ic  = u[x, 0] == k*Exp[x - 1]*HeavisideTheta[x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, ic}, u[x, t], {x, t}, Assumptions -> k > 0], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 97*)


pde =  D[v[t, s], t] + (1*sigma^2*s^2*D[v[t, s], {s, 2}])/2 + (r - q)*s*D[v[t, s], s] - r*v[t, s] == 0;
bc  = v[T, s] == psi[s];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, bc}, v[t, s], {t, s}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 98*)


pde =  D[u[x, t], {x, 3}] + D[u[x, t], {t}] - 6*u[x, t]*D[u[x, t], {x}] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, u[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 99*)


pde =  D[u[x, y], {x, 2}] + y*D[u[x, y], {y, 2}] == 0;
bc  = {u[x, 0] == 0, Derivative[0, 1][u][x, 0] == x^2};
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, bc}, u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 100*)


pde =  D[u[x, y], {x, 2}] + x*D[u[x, y], {y, 2}] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 101*)


pde =  x*D[u[x, y], {x, 2}] + D[u[x, y], {y, 2}] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 102*)


pde =  D[u[x, y], {x, 2}] + D[u[x, y], {y, 2}] + beta/x*D[u[x,y],x] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, u[x, y], {x, y}, Assumptions->beta>0], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 103*)


pde =  D[u[x, y], {x, 2}] - D[u[x, y], {y, 2}] + beta/x*D[u[x,y],x] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, u[x, y], {x, y}, Assumptions->beta>0], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 104*)


pde =  D[u[x, t], {t, 2}] - D[u[x, t], {x, 2}] - 2/x*D[u[x,t],x] == 0;
ic = {u[x,0]==0,Derivative[0,1][u][x,0]==g[x]};
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, ic}, u[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 105*)


pde =  D[u[theta, v], {theta, 2}] + v^2/(1-v^2/c^2)* D[u[theta,v],{v,2}]+v*D[u[theta,v],v]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, u[theta, v], {theta, v}, Assumptions->c>0], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 106*)

ClearAll[u, v, x, y];
 pde1 = D[u[x, y], x] == D[v[x, y], y];
 pde2 = D[u[x, y], y] == -D[v[x, y], x];
bc  = {u[x, 0] == x^3, v[x, 0] == 0};
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde1, pde2, bc}, {u[x, y], v[x, y]}, {x, y}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 107*)

ClearAll[u, v, x, y];
 pde1 = D[u[x, y], x] == D[v[x, y], y];
 pde2 = D[u[x, y], y] == -D[v[x, y], x] + y;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde1, pde2}, {u[x, y], v[x, y]}, {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 108*)


pde =  -D[s[t, \[Zeta], \[Eta], \[Phi]], t] == ((\[Zeta]^2 - 1)*D[s[t, \[Zeta], \[Eta], \[Phi]], \[Zeta]]^2)/(2*\[Sigma]^2*m*(-\[Eta]^2 + \[Zeta]^2)) + ((-\[Eta]^2 - 1)*D[s[t, \[Zeta], \[Eta], \[Phi]], \[Eta]]^2)/(2*\[Sigma]^2*m*(-\[Eta]^2 + \[Zeta]^2)) + (1*D[s[t, \[Zeta], \[Eta], \[Phi]], \[Phi]]^2)/(2*\[Sigma]^2*m*(\[Zeta]^2 - 1)*(-\[Eta]^2 - 1)) + (a[\[Zeta]] + b[\[Zeta]])/(-\[Eta]^2 + \[Zeta]^2);
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, s[t, \[Zeta], \[Eta], \[Phi]], {t, \[Zeta], \[Eta], \[Phi]}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 109*)


pde =  D[u[x, t], t] + D[u[x, t], {x,3}] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, u[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 110*)


pde =  D[u[x, t], t] + u[x, t]*D[u[x, t], x] == v*D[u[x, t], {x, 2}];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, u[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 111*)


pde =  D[u[x, t], t] + D[u[x, t], x] + u[x, t]*D[u[x, t], x] - D[D[u[x, t], {x, 2}], t] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, u[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 112*)


pde =  D[u[x, t], t] + h*D[u[x, t], {x, 2}] + u[x, t]*D[u[x, t], x] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, u[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 113*)


pde =  (1 - D[u[x, t], t]^2)*D[u[x, t], {x, 2}] + 2*D[u[x, t], x]*D[u[x, t], t]*D[D[u[x, t], x], t] - (1 + D[u[x, t], x]^2)*D[u[x, t], {t, 2}] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, u[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 114*)


pde =  D[u[x, t], {t, 2}] - D[u[x, t], {x, 2}] - D[u[x, t], {x, 4}] - 3*D[u[x, t]^2, {x, 2}] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, u[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 115*)


pde =  D[u[x, t], {t, 2}] - D[u[x, t], {x, 2}] - D[u[x, t], {x, 4}] - 3*D[u[x, t]^2, {x, 2}] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, u[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 116*)


pde =  D[u[x, t], t] == D[u[x, t]^4, {x, 2}] + D[u[x, t]^3, x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, u[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 117*)


pde =  D[u[x, t], t] + 2*k*D[u[x, t], x] - D[D[u[x, t], {x, 2}], t] + 3*u[x, t]*D[u[x, t], x] == 2*D[u[x, t], x]*D[u[x, t], {x, 2}] + u[x, t]*D[u[x, t], {x, 3}];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, u[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 118*)


pde =  D[u[x, t], t] - D[u[x, t], {x, 2}] + lambda*(u[x, t]^3 - u[x, t]) == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, u[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 119*)


pde =  D[D[theta[x, t], t] - gamma*Exp[theta[x, t]], {t, 2}] == D[D[theta[x, t], t] - Exp[theta[x, t]], {x, 2}];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, theta[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 120*)


pde =  D[u[x, t], t] - D[D[u[x, t], {x, 2}], t] + 4*u[x, t]*D[u[x, t], x] == 3*D[u[x, t], x]*D[u[x, t], {x, 2}] + u[x, t]*D[u[x, t], {x, 3}];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, u[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 121*)


pde =  D[u[x, t], t] == u[x, t]^3*D[u[x, t], {x, 3}];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, u[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 122*)


pde =  D[D[u[x, y, t], t], {y, 3}] + beta*D[u[x, y, t], y]*D[D[u[x, y, t], y], t] + beta*D[u[x, y, t], {y, 2}]*D[u[x, y, t], t] + D[u[x, y, t], {t, 2}] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, u[x, y, t], {x, y, t}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 123*)


pde =  D[u[x, t], t] == u[x, t]*(1 - u[x, t]) + D[u[x, t], {x, 2}];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, u[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 124*)


pde =  D[D[u[x, t], t] + u[x, t]*D[u[x, t], x], x] == (1*D[u[x, t], x]^2)/2;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, u[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 125*)


pde =  D[D[u[x, y, t], t] + u[x, y, t]*D[u[x, y, t], x] + eps^2*D[u[x, y, t], {x, 3}], t] + lambda*D[u[x, y, t], {y, 2}] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, u[x, y, t], {x, y, t}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 126*)


pde =  Laplacian[u[x, y], {x, y}] + lambda*u[x, y]^p == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 127*)


pde =  Laplacian[u[x, y], {x, y}] + u[x, y]^2 == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 128*)


pde =  D[D[u[x, y, t], x], t] - D[u[x, y, t]*D[u[x, y, t], x], x] == D[u[x, y, t], {y, 2}];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, u[x, y, t], {x, y, t}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 129*)


pde =  D[u[x, t], t] + D[u[x, t], {x,3}] + 6*u[x, t]*D[u[x, t], x] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, u[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 130*)


pde =  2*D[u[x, y, t], t, x] + D[u[x, y, t], x]*D[u[x, y, t], {x, 2}] - D[u[x, y, t], {y, 2}] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, u[x, y, t], {x, y, t}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 131*)


pde =  Laplacian[u[x, y], {x, y}] + Exp[lam*u[x, y]] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 132*)


pde =  (1 + D[u[x, y], y]^2)*D[u[x, y], {x, 2}] - 2*D[u[x, y], x]*D[u[x, y], y]*D[u[x, y], x, y] + (1 + D[u[x, y], x]^2)*D[u[x, y], {y, 2}] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 133*)


pde =  D[u[x, t], {t, 2}] - D[u[x, t], {x, 2}] == epsilon*(D[u[x, t], t] - D[u[x, t], t]^3);
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, u[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 134*)


pde =  D[u[x, t], t] + 45*u[x, t]^2*D[u[x, t], x] + 15*D[u[x, t], x]*D[u[x, t], {x, 2}] + 15*u[x, t]*D[u[x, t], {x, 3}] + D[u[x, t], {x, 5}] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, u[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 135*)


pde =  D[phi[x, t], {t, 2}] - D[phi[x, t], {x, 2}] + Sin[phi[x, t]] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, phi[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 136*)


pde =  D[u[x, t], x, t] == Sinh[u[x, t]];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, u[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 137*)


pde =  Laplacian[u[x, y], {x, y}] + Sinh[u[x, y]] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 138*)


pde =  D[u[x, y], x, y] + alpha*D[u[x, y], x] + beta*D[u[x, y], y] + nu*D[u[x, y], x]*D[u[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 139*)


pde =  D[phi[x, t], t, t] - D[phi[x, t], x, x] - phi[x, t] + phi[x, t]^3 == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, phi[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 140*)


pde =  s[x, y]*D[s[x, y], x, y] + D[s[x, y], x]*D[s[x, y], y] == 1;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, s[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 141*)


pde =  D[u[r, theta], {r, 2}] + D[u[r, theta], {theta, 2}] == 0;
bc  = u[2, theta] == 3*Sin[2*theta] + 1;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, bc}, u[r, theta], {r, theta}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 142*)


pde =  D[u[x, y], {x, 2}] + y*D[u[x, y], {y, 2}] == 0;
bc  = {u[x, 0] == 0, Derivative[0, 1][u][x, 0] == x^2};
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, bc}, u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 143*)


pde =  D[u[x, t], t] == -D[u[x, t], {x, 3}];
ic  = u[x, 0] == f[x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, ic}, u[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 144*)


pde =  D[u[x, y], y, x] == Sin[x]*Sin[y];
bc  = {u[x, 0] == 1 + Cos[x], Derivative[0, 1][u][0, y] == -2*Sin[y]};
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, bc}, u[x, y], x, y], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 145*)


pde =  D[w[x1, x2, x3, t], t] == D[w[x1, x2, x3, t], {x1, 2}] + D[w[x1, x2, x3, t], {x2, 2}] + D[w[x1, x2, x3, t], {x3, 2}];
ic  = w[x1, x2, x3, 1] == Exp[a]*x1^2 + x2*x3;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, ic}, w[x1, x2, x3, t], {x1, x2, x3, t}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 146*)


pde =  D[w[x1, x2, x3, t], t] == D[w[x1, x2, x3, t], x1, x2] + D[w[x1, x2, x3, t], x1, x3] + D[w[x1, x2, x3, t], {x3, 2}] - D[w[x1, x2, x3, t], x2, x3];
ic  = w[x1, x2, x3, t0] == Exp[x1] + x2 - 3*x3;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, ic}, w[x1, x2, x3, t], {x1, x2, x3, t}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 147*)


pde =  D[w[x1, x2, x3, t], {t, 2}] == D[w[x1, x2, x3, t], x1, x2] + D[w[x1, x2, x3, t], x1, x3] + D[w[x1, x2, x3, t], {x3, 2}] - D[w[x1, x2, x3, t], x2, x3];
ic  = {w[x1, x2, x3, t0] == x1^3*x2^2 + x3, Derivative[0, 0, 0, 1][w][x1, x2, x3, t0] == -(x2*x3) + x1};
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, ic}, w[x1, x2, x3, t], {x1, x2, x3, t}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 148*)


pde =  D[u[x, t], t] == beta*D[u[x, t], x] + d*D[u[x, t], {x, 2}];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, u[x, t], {x, t}, Assumptions -> {beta > 0, d > 0, x > 0, t > 0}], 60*10]];

sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, bc, ic}, u[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];

sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, ic}, u[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];

sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, ic}, u[x, t], {x, t}, Assumptions -> {t > 0, k > 0}], 60*10]];
 sol[[2]] = sol[[2]] /. K[1] -> s;

ic  = u[x, y, 0] == x*(1 - x)*(1 - y)*y;

(*PDE number 295*)


pde =  Laplacian[u[x, y], {x, y}] == 0;
bc  = {u[0, y] == 0, u[Pi, y] == Sinh[Pi]*Cos[y], u[x, 0] == Sin[x], u[x, Pi] == -Sinh[x]};
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, bc}, u[x, y], x, y], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 296*)


pde =  D[u[x, y], {x, 2}] + D[u[x, y], {y, 2}] == 0;
bc  = u[0, y] == Sin[y]/y;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, bc}, u[x, y], {x, y}, Assumptions -> 0 < x], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 297*)


pde =  D[u[x, y], {x, 2}] + D[u[x, y], {y, 2}] == 0;
bc  = {u[x, 0] == 0, u[x, a] == 0, u[0, y] == Sin[y]};
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, bc}, u[x, y], {x, y}, Assumptions -> a > 0], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 298*)


pde =  Laplacian[u[x, y], {x, y}] == 0;
bc  = u[x, 0] == UnitBox[x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, bc}, u[x, y], {x, y},Assumptions -> {y > 0, -Infinity < x < Infinity}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 299*)


pde =  Laplacian[u[x, y], {x, y}] == 0;
bc  = u[0, y] == Sinc[y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, bc}, u[x, y], {x, y},Assumptions -> {x > 0, -Infinity < y < Infinity}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 300*)


pde =  Laplacian[u[x, y], {x, y}] == 0;
bc  = {u[x, 0] == -((x - 2)^2 + 3)^(-1), u[0, y] == 1/((y - 3)^2 + 1)};
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, bc}, u[x, y], {x, y},Assumptions -> {x > 0, y > 0}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 301*)


pde =  Laplacian[u[x, y], {x, y}] == 0;
bc  = Derivative[0, 1][u][x, 0] == UnitBox[x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, bc}, u[x, y], {x, y},Assumptions -> y>0], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 302*)


pde =  Laplacian[u[x, y], {x, y}] == 0;
bc  = {u[x, 0] == x^2*(1 - x), u[x, 2] == 0, u[0, y] == 0, u[1, y] == 0};
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, bc}, u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];
sol =  sol /. K[1] -> n
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 303*)


pde =  Laplacian[u[x, y], {x, y}] == 0;
bc  = {u[x, 0] == 0, u[x, b] == h[x]};
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, bc}, u[x, y], {x, y}, Assumptions -> {0 < y < b, b > 0}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 304*)


pde =  Laplacian[u[x, y], {x, y}] == 0;
bc  = {u[x, 0] == 0, u[x, a] == 0, u[0, y] == Sin[y]};
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, bc}, u[x, y], {x, y}, Assumptions -> a > 0], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 305*)


pde =  D[u[r, theta], {r, 2}] + (1*D[u[r, theta], r])/r + (1*D[u[r, theta], {theta, 2}])/r^2 == 0;
 bcOnR = {Derivative[1, 0][u][1, theta] == f[theta]};
 bcOnTheta = {u[r, 0] == 0, u[r, Pi/2] == 0};
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, bcOnR, bcOnTheta}, u[r, theta], {r, theta}, Assumptions -> {r > 0, r < 1, theta > 0, theta < Pi/2}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 306*)


pde =  D[u[r, theta], {r, 2}] + (D[u[r, theta], r])/r + (D[u[r, theta], {theta, 2}])/r^2 == 0;
bc  = u[4, theta] == (4*Cos[theta])^4;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, bc}, u[r, theta], {r, theta}], 60*10]];
sol =  sol /. K[1] -> n;
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 307*)

pde = Laplacian[u[r, theta], {r, theta}, "Polar"] == 0;
bc = {Derivative[1, 0][u][1, theta] == Cos[theta], u[r, -Pi] == u[r, Pi], Derivative[0, 1][u][r, -Pi] == Derivative[0, 1][u][r, Pi]};
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, bc}, u[r, theta], {r, theta}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 308*)


pde =  D[u[r, theta], {r, 2}] + (1*D[u[r, theta], r])/r + (1*D[u[r, theta], {theta, 2}])/r^2 == 0;
bc  = u[a, theta] == f[theta];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, bc}, u[r, theta], {r, theta}, Assumptions -> a < r && a > 0 && Inequality[0, Less, theta, LessEqual, 2*Pi]], 60*10]];
sol =  sol /. K[1] -> n;
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 309*)


a = 1;
pde = Laplacian[u[r, theta], {r, theta}, "Polar"] == 0;
f[theta_] := 1/4*(Cos[theta] - Cos[3*theta]);
bc = {u[a, theta] == f[theta], u[r, -Pi] == u[r, Pi], Derivative[0, 1][u][r, -Pi] == Derivative[0, 1][u][r, Pi]};
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, bc}, u[r, theta], {r, theta}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 310*)


pde =  D[u[r, theta], {r, 2}] + (1*D[u[r, theta], r]*1*D[u[r, theta], {theta, 2}])/(r*r^2) == 0;
bc  = {Derivative[1, 0][u][1, theta] == f[theta], u[r, Pi/2] == 0, u[r, 0] == 0};
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, bc}, u[r, theta], {r, theta}, Assumptions -> {0 <= r <= 1 && 0 <= theta <= Pi/2}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 311*)

bc  = {u[r, 0] == 0, u[r, Pi] == 0, u[1, theta] == f[theta]};

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 312*)

bc  = {Derivative[1, 0][u][a, theta] == 0, u[b, theta] == g[theta]};

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 313*)

bc  = {u[1, theta] == 0, u[2, theta] == Sin[theta]};
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, bc}, u[r, theta], {r, theta}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 314*)

bc  = {u[a, theta] == f[theta], u[r, -Pi] == u[r, Pi], Derivative[0, 1][u][r, -Pi] == Derivative[0, 1][u][r, Pi]};

(*PDE number 331*)

bc  = {u[x, 0] == 0, u[x, Pi] == 0, u[0, y] == 1, u[Pi, y] == 0};

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 332*)


U = u[r, theta, t]; 
pde = D[U, t] == k*(lambda*U + (1/r^2)*D[r^2*D[U, r], r] + (1/(r^2*Sin[theta]))*D[Sin[theta]*D[U, theta], theta]); 
bc  = u[R, theta, t] == 0; 
sol = AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, bc}, U, {r, theta, t}, Assumptions -> {k > 0, r < R}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 333*)


pde =  D[u[x, t], {t, 2}] == c^2*D[u[x, t], {x, 2}];
ic  = {u[x, 0] == f[x], Derivative[0, 1][u][x, 0] == g[x]};
bc  = {u[0, t] == 0, u[L, t] == 0};
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, ic, bc}, u[x, t], {x, t}, Assumptions->L>0], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 334*)


L=10; c=2; f=0; g=(8*x*(L-x)^2)/L^3;
pde =  D[u[x, t], {t, 2}] == c^2*D[u[x, t], {x, 2}];
ic  = {u[x, 0] == f, Derivative[0, 1][u][x, 0] == g};

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 335*)


L=10; c=2; g=0; f=(8*x*(L-x)^2)/L^3;

sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, bc, ic}, u[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 349*)


gamma=1/8; c=1; L=10;
f=Piecewise[{{x-4,4<=x<=5},{6-x,5<x<=6},{0,True}}];
pde =  D[u[x, t], {t, 2}] + gamma^2*u[x, t] == c^2*D[u[x, t], {x, 2}];
bc  = {u[0, t] == 0, u[L, t] == 0};
ic  = {Derivative[0, 1][u][x, 0] == 0, u[x, 0] == f};
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, bc, ic}, u[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 350*)


pde =  D[u[x, t], {t, 2}] == 4*D[u[x, t], {x, 2}];
ic  = {Derivative[0, 1][u][x, 0] == 0, u[x, 0] == Sin[x]^2};
bc  = {u[0, t] == 0, u[Pi, t] == 0};
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, bc, ic}, u[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];
sol =  sol /. K[1] -> n;
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 351*)


pde =  D[u[x, t], {t, 2}] == D[u[x, t], {x, 2}] + x*Exp[-t];
ic  = {u[x, 0] == 0, Derivative[0, 1][u][x, 0] == 0};
bc  = {u[0, t] == 0, u[1, t] == 0};
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, bc, ic}, u[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)

bc  = {u[0, t] == 0, Derivative[1, 0][u][L, t] == 0};
ic  = {Derivative[0, 1][u][x, 0] == g[x], u[x, 0] == f[x]};
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, bc, ic}, u[x, t], {x, t}, Assumptions -> L>0], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)

bc  = {u[0, t] == 0, Derivative[1, 0][u][L, t] == 0};

bc  = {u[0, t] == 0, Derivative[1, 0][u][1, t] == 0};
ic  = {u[x, 0] == x^2 - 2*x, u[x, 1] == u[x, 1/2] + ((1/2)*x^2 - x)*Exp[-1] - ((3*x^2)/4 - (3/2)*x)*Exp[-2^(-1)]};

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 396*)


L=2;
H=3;
c=1/3;
f1[x_] :=Piecewise[{{x, x < L/2}, {L - x, x > L/2}}];
f2[y_] := Piecewise[{{y, y < H/2}, {H - y, y > H/2}}];
pde =  D[u[x, y, t], {t, 2}] == c^2 * Laplacian[u[x, y, t], {x, y}];
ic  = {u[x, y, 0] == 3*f1[x]*f2[y], Derivative[0, 0, 1][u][x, y, 0] == 0};
bc  = {u[x, 0, t] == 0, u[0, y, t] == 0, u[L, y, t] == 0, u[x, H, t] == 0};
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, ic, bc}, u[x, y, t], {x, y, t}], 60*10]];
sol =  sol /. {K[1] -> n, K[2] -> m};

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 398*)


pde =  D[u[x, y, t], {t, 2}] == Laplacian[u[x, y, t], {x, y}];
ic  = {u[x, y, 0] == (1/10)*(x - x^2)*(2*y - y^2), Derivative[0, 0, 1][u][x, y, 0] == 0};
bc  = {u[x, 0, t] == 0, u[0, y, t] == 0, u[1, y, t] == 0, u[x, 2, t] == 0};
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, ic, bc}, u[x, y, t], {x, y, t}], 60*10]];
sol =  sol /. {K[1] -> n, K[2] -> m};
sol =  Assuming[Element[{n, m}, Integers], FullSimplify[sol]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 399*)


pde =  D[u[x, y, t], {t, 2}] == c^2*Laplacian[u[x, y, t], {x, y}] + Q[x, y, t];
ic  = {u[x, y, 0] == f[x, y], Derivative[0, 0, 1][u][x, y, 0] == 0};
bc  = {u[0, y, t] == 0, u[L, y, t] == 0, u[x, 0, t] == 0, u[x, H, t] == 0};
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, ic, bc}, u[x, y, t], {x, y, t}, Assumptions -> {L > 0, H > 0, t > 0, c > 0}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 400*)


pde =  D[u[x, y, t], {t, 2}] == (1*(D[u[x, y, t], {x, 2}] + D[u[x, y, t], {y, 2}]))/4;
ic  = {Derivative[0, 0, 1][u][x, y, 0] == 0, u[x, y, 0] == x*y*(Pi - y)};
bc  = {Derivative[1, 0, 0][u][0, y, t] == 0, Derivative[1, 0, 0][u][Pi, y, t] == 0, u[x, 0, t] == 0, u[x, Pi, t] == 0};
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, bc, ic}, u[x, y, t], {x, y, t}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 401*)


pde =  D[u[x, y, t], {t, 2}] == c^2 * Laplacian[u[x, y, t], {x, y}];
ic  = {u[x, y, 0] == f[x,y], Derivative[0, 0, 1][u][x, y, 0] == 0};
bc  = {u[x, 0, t] == 0, u[0, y, t] == 0, u[L, y, t] == 0, u[x, H, t] == 0};
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, ic, bc}, u[x, y, t], {x, y, t}], 60*10]];
sol =  sol /. {K[1] -> n, K[2] -> m};
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 402*)


pde =  D[u[x, y, t], {t, 2}] == (1*(D[u[x, y, t], {x, 2}] + D[u[x, y, t], {y, 2}]))/4 - (1*D[u[x, y, t], t])/10;
ic  = {u[x, y, 0] == 0, Derivative[0, 0, 1][u][x, y, 0] == x*(1 - (1/2)*x)*(1 - (1/2)*y)*y};
bc  = {u[0, y, t] == 0, Derivative[1, 0, 0][u][1, y, t] == 0, u[x, 0, t] == 0, Derivative[0, 1, 0][u][x, 1, t] == 0};
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, bc, ic}, u[x, y, t], {x, y, t}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 403*)


 ode = D[u[x, y], {x, 2}] - 2*Sin[x]*D[u[x, y], x, y] - Cos[x]^2*D[u[x, y], {y, 2}] - Cos[x]*D[u[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[ode, u[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 404*)


pde =  D[u[r, t], {t, 2}] == c^2*(D[u[r, t], {r, 2}] + 1/r*D[u[r, t], r]);
ic  = {u[r, 0] == f[r], Derivative[0, 1][u][r, 0] == g[r]};
bc  = u[a, t] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, ic, bc}, u[r, t], {r, t},Assumptions->{t>0,r>0,r<a}], 60*10]];
sol =  sol /. K[1] -> n;
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 405*)


pde =  D[u[r, t], {t, 2}] == c^2*(D[u[r, t], {r, 2}] + 1/r*D[u[r, t], r]);
ic  = {u[r, 0] == 1, Derivative[0, 1][u][r, 0] == r/3};
bc  = u[1, t] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, ic, bc}, u[r, t], {r, t}], 60*10]];
sol =  sol /. K[1] -> n;
sol =  FullSimplify[sol];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 406*)


c=2/10; a=1; 
g[r_]:=0;
f[r_]:=r;
pde =  D[u[r, t], {t, 2}] == c^2*(D[u[r, t], {r, 2}] + 1/r*D[u[r, t], r]);
ic  = {u[r, 0] == f[r], Derivative[0, 1][u][r, 0] == g[r]};
bc  = u[a, t] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, ic, bc}, u[r, t], {r, t},Assumptions->{t>0,r>0}], 60*10]];
sol =  sol /. K[1] -> n;

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 407*)


pde = D[u[r, t], {r, 2}] + D[u[r, t], r]/r + D[u[r, t], {t, 2}] == -Q0*q[r];
ic = u[r, 0] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, ic}, u[r, t], {r, t}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 408*)


pde = c^2*(D[u[r, t], {r, 2}] + D[u[r, t], r]/r) == D[u[r, t], {t, 2}];
ic = {u[r, 0] == A*a*(a^2 + r^2)^(-1/2), Derivative[0, 1][u][r, 0] == 0};
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, ic}, u[r, t], {r, t}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 409*)


pde =  D[u[r, theta, t], {t, 2}] == c^2*Laplacian[u[r,theta,t],{r,theta},"Polar"];
ic  = {u[r, theta, 0] == f[r, theta], Derivative[0, 0, 1][u][r, theta, 0] == g[r,theta]};
bc  = {u[a, theta, t] == 0, u[r, -Pi, t] == u[r, Pi, t], Derivative[0, 1, 0][u][r, -Pi, t] == Derivative[0, 1, 0][u][r, Pi, t]};
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, ic, bc}, u[r, theta, t], {r, theta, t}, Assumptions -> {0 < r < a, a > 0, t > 0, -Pi < theta < Pi}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 410*)


pde =  D[u[r, theta, t], {t, 2}] == c^2*Laplacian[u[r,theta,t],{r,theta},"Polar"];
ic  = {u[r, theta, 0] == f[r, theta], Derivative[0, 0, 1][u][r, theta, 0] == 0};
bc  = {u[a, theta, t] == 0, u[r, -Pi, t] == u[r, Pi, t], Derivative[0, 1, 0][u][r, -Pi, t] == Derivative[0, 1, 0][u][r, Pi, t]};
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, ic, bc}, u[r, theta, t], {r, theta, t}, Assumptions -> {0 < r < a, a > 0, t > 0, -Pi < theta < Pi}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 411*)


f[r_,theta_]:=r*theta;
c=2/10; a=1;
pde =  D[u[r, theta, t], {t, 2}] == c^2*Laplacian[u[r,theta,t],{r,theta},"Polar"];
ic  = {u[r, theta, 0] == f[r, theta], Derivative[0, 0, 1][u][r, theta, 0] == 0};
bc  = {u[a, theta, t] == 0, u[r, -Pi, t] == u[r, Pi, t], Derivative[0, 1, 0][u][r, -Pi, t] == Derivative[0, 1, 0][u][r, Pi, t]};
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, ic, bc}, u[r, theta, t], {r, theta, t}, Assumptions -> {0 < r < a,t > 0, -Pi < theta < Pi}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 412*)


a=1; c=1; epsilon=1/2;
f[r_,theta_]:=0;
g[r_,theta_]:=Piecewise[{{1/(Pi*epsilon^2),r<epsilon},{0,True}}];
c=1; a=1;
pde =  D[u[r, theta, t], {t, 2}] == c^2*Laplacian[u[r,theta,t],{r,theta},"Polar"];
ic  = {u[r, theta, 0] == f[r, theta], Derivative[0, 0, 1][u][r, theta, 0] == g[r,theta]};
bc  = {u[a, theta, t] == 0, u[r, -Pi, t] == u[r, Pi, t], Derivative[0, 1, 0][u][r, -Pi, t] == Derivative[0, 1, 0][u][r, Pi, t]};
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, ic, bc}, u[r, theta, t], {r, theta, t}, Assumptions -> {0 < r < a,t > 0, -Pi < theta < Pi}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 413*)


pde =  D[u[r, theta, t], {t, 2}] == c^2*Laplacian[u[r,theta,t],{r,theta},"Polar"]+Q[r,theta,t];
ic  = {u[r, theta, 0] == f[r, theta], Derivative[0, 0, 1][u][r, theta, 0] == 0};
bc  = {u[a, theta, t] == 0, u[r, -Pi, t] == u[r, Pi, t], Derivative[0, 1, 0][u][r, -Pi, t] == Derivative[0, 1, 0][u][r, Pi, t]};
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[{pde, ic, bc}, u[r, theta, t], {r, theta, t}, Assumptions -> {0 < r < a,t > 0, -Pi < theta < Pi}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 414*)


 lap = Laplacian[u[r, theta, phi, t], {r, theta, phi}, "Spherical"];
pde =  D[u[r, theta, phi, t], {t, 2}] == c^2*lap;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, u[r, theta, phi, t], {r, theta, phi, t}, Assumptions -> {0 < theta < Pi}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)

lap = Laplacian[u[r, phi, z, t], {r, phi, z}, "Cylindrical"];

sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, u[r, phi, z, t], {r, phi, z, t}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 416*)

pde =  D[w[x, y], x] == f[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)

sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)

pde =  D[w[x, y], x] == w[x, y]*f[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)

(*----------------------------*)

(*----------------------------*)

sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 422*)


pde =  a*D[w[x, y], x] + b*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 423*)


pde =  a*D[w[x, y], x] + (b*x + c)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 424*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*x + b*y + c)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 425*)


pde =  a*x*D[w[x, y], x] + b*y*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 426*)


pde =  a*y*D[w[x, y], x] + b*x*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 427*)


pde =  y*D[w[x, y], x] + (y + a)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 428*)


pde =  (a*y + b*x + c)*D[w[x, y], x] - (b*y + k*x + s)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 429*)


pde =  (a1*x + b1*y + c1)*D[w[x, y], x] + (a1*x + b2*y + c2)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 430*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*x^2 + b*x + c)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 431*)

pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*y^2 + b*y + c)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 432*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*y + b*x^2 + c*x)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)

sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];


(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 434*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (y^2 - a^2*x^2 + 3*a)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 435*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (y^2 - a^2*x^2 + a)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 436*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (y^2 + a*x*y + a)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 437*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (y^2 + a*x*y - a*b*x - b^2)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 438*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + k*(a*x + a*y + c)^2*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 439*)


pde =  x*D[w[x, y], x] + (a*y^2 + c*x^2 + y)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 440*)

pde =  x*D[w[x, y], x] + (a*y^2 + b*x*y + c*x^2 + y)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 441*)


pde =  (a*x + c)*D[w[x, y], x] + (alpha*(a*y + b*x)^2 + beta*(a*y + b*x) - b*x + gamma)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 442*)


pde =  a*x^2*D[w[x, y], x] + b*y^2*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 443*)


pde =  (a*x^2 + b)*D[w[x, y], x] - (y^2 - 2*x*y + (1 - a)*x^2 - b)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 444*)

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 445*)


pde =  (x - a)*(x - b)*D[w[x, y], x] - (y^2 + k*(y + x - a)*(y + x - b))*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 446*)

sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 447*)


pde =  y*(a*x + b)*D[w[x, y], x] + (a*y^2 - c*x)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)

sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)

sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 450*)

(*----------------------------*)

(*----------------------------*)

sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];
