sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 456*)


pde =  (A*y^2 + B*x*y + C0*x^2 + k*x)*D[w[x, y], x] + (D0*y^2 + E0*x*y + F0*x^2 + k*y)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 457*)


pde =  (A*y^2 + B*x*y + C0*x^2)*D[w[x, y], x] + (D0*y^2 + E0*x*y + F0*x^2)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 458*)


pde =  (A*y^2 + 2*B*x*y + D0*x^2 + a)*D[w[x, y], x] - (D0*y^2 + 2*D0*x*y - E0*x^2 - b)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 459*)


pde =  (y^2 - 2*x*y + x^2 + a*y)*D[w[x, y], x] + a*y*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 460*)


pde =  (x*(a1 + b1*x + c1*y) - (a2 + b2*x + c2*y))*D[w[x, y], x] + (y*(a1 + b1*x + c1*y) - (a3 + b3*x + c3*y))*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 461*)

pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (y^2 + b*x^2*y - a^2 - a*b*x)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 462*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*x^2*y + b*x^3 + c)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 463*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*x^2*y + b*y^3)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 464*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*x*y + b)*y^2*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 465*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + A*(a*x + b*y + c)^3*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 466*)


pde =  x*D[w[x, y], x] + (a*x^4*y^3 + (b*x^2 - 1)*y + c*x)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 467*)


pde =  x^2*D[w[x, y], x] + (a*x^2*y^2 + b*x*y + c)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 468*)


pde =  (a*x^2*y + b)*D[w[x, y], x] - (a*x*y^2 + c)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 469*)


pde =  (a*x + b*y^3)*D[w[x, y], x] - (c*x^3 + a*y)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 470*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*Sqrt[x]*y)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 471*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*Sqrt[x]*y + b*Sqrt[y])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 472*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*Sqrt[x]*y + b*x*Sqrt[y])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 473*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + A*Sqrt[a*x + b*y + c]*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 474*)


pde =  x*D[w[x, y], x] + (a*y + b*Sqrt[y^2 + c*x^2])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 475*)


pde =  (a*x + b*Sqrt[y])*D[w[x, y], x] - (c*Sqrt[x] + a*y)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 476*)


 f[t_] := Sum[a[n]*t^n, {n, 1, 4}];
pde =  Sqrt[f[x]]*D[w[x, y], x] + Sqrt[f[y]]*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 477*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*y + b*x^k)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 478*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*x^k*y + b*x^n)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];
sol = Simplify[sol];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 479*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*y^2 + b*x^n)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];
 sol=Simplify[sol];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 480*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (y^2 + a*n*x^(n - 1) - a^2*x^(2*n))*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 481*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (y^2 + a*x^n*y + a*x^(n - 1))*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];
sol = Simplify[sol];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 482*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (y^2 + a*x^n*y - a*b*x^n - b^2)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 483*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*x^n*y^2 + b*x^(-n - 2))*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 484*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*x^n*y^2 + b*m*x^(m - 1) - a*b^2*x^(n + 2*m))*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 485*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + ((n + 1)*x^n*y^2 - a*x^(n + m + 1)*y + a*x^m)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 486*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*x^n*y^2 + b*x^m*y + b*c*x^m - a*c^2*x^n)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 487*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*x^n*y^2 - a*x^n*(b*x^m + c)*y + b*m*x^(m - 1))*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 488*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] - (a*n*x^(n - 1)*y^2 - c*x^m*(a*x^n + b) + c*x^m)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 489*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*x^n*y^2 + b*x^m*y + c*k*x^(k - 1) - b*c*x^(m + k) - a*c^2*x^(n + 2*k))*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 490*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*x^(2*n + 1)*y^3 + b*x^(-n - 2))*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 491*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*x^n*y^3 + 3*a*b*x^(n + m)*y^2 - b*m*x^(m - 1) - 2*a*b^3*x^(n + 3*m))*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 492*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*x^n*y^3 + 3*a*b*x^(n + m)*y^2 + c*x^k*y - 2*a*b^3*x^(n + 3*m) + b*c*x^(m + l) - b*m*x^(m - 1))*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 493*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*y^n + b*x^(n/(1 - n)))*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 494*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*x^(m - n - m*n)*y^n + b*x^m)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 495*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*x^n*y^k + b*x^m*y)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 496*)


pde =  x*D[w[x, y], x] + (a*y^2 + b*y + c*x^(2*b))*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 497*)


pde =  x*D[w[x, y], x] + (a*y^2 + (n + b*x^n)*y + c*x^(2*n))*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 498*)

pde =  x*D[w[x, y], x] + (a*x^n*y^2 + b*y + c/x^n)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 499*)


pde =  x*D[w[x, y], x] + (a*x^n*y^2 + m*y - a*b^2*x^(x + 2*m))*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 500*)


pde =  x*D[w[x, y], x] + (x^(2*n)*y^2 + (m - n)*y + x^(2*m))*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 501*)


pde =  x*D[w[x, y], x] + (a*x^(2*n)*y^2 + (b*x^n - n)*y + c)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 502*)


pde =  x*D[w[x, y], x] + (a*x^(2*n + m)*y^2 + (b*x^(n + m) - n)*y + c*x^m)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 503*)


pde =  x*D[w[x, y], x] + (a*y^3 + 3*a*b*x^n*y^2 - b*n*x^n - 2*a*b^3*x^(3*n))*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 504*)


pde =  x*D[w[x, y], x] + (a*x^(2*n + 1)*y^3 + (b*x - n)*y + c*x^(1 - n))*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 505*)


pde =  x*D[w[x, y], x] + (a*x^(n + 2)*y^3 + (b*x^n - 1)*y + c*x^(n - 1))*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 506*)


pde =  x*D[w[x, y], x] + (y + a*x^(n - m)*y^m + b*x^(n - k)*y^k)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 507*)


pde =  y*D[w[x, y], x] + (x^(n - 1)*((1 + 2*n)*x + a*n)*y - n*x^(2*n)*(x + a))*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 508*)


pde =  y*D[w[x, y], x] + ((a*(2*n + k)*x^k + b)*x^(n - 1)*y - (a^2*n*x^(2*k) + a*b*x^k - c)*x^(2*n - 1))*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 509*)


pde =  x*(2*a*x*y + b)*D[w[x, y], x] - (a*(m + 3)*x*y^2 + b*(m + 2)*y - c*x^m)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 510*)


pde =  x^2*(2*a*x*y + b)*D[w[x, y], x] - (4*a*x^2*y^2 + 3*b*x*y - c*x^2 - k)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 511*)


pde =  a*x^m*D[w[x, y], x] + b*y^n*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 512*)


pde =  a*x^n*D[w[x, y], x] + (b*y + c*x^m)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 513*)


pde =  a*x^k*D[w[x, y], x] + (y^n + b*x^m*y)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}, Assumptions -> {n != 1}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 514*)

pde =  x*(a*x^k + b)*D[w[x, y], x] + (alpha*x^n*y^2 + (beta - a*n*x^k)*y + gamma/x^n)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 515*)


pde =  (y + A*x^n + a)*D[w[x, y], x] - (n*A*x^(n - 1)*y + k*x^m + b)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 516*)


pde =  (y + a*x^(n + 1) + b*x^n)*D[w[x, y], x] + (a*n*x^n + c*x^(n - 1))*y*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 517*)


pde =  x*(2*a*x^n*y + b)*D[w[x, y], x] - (a*(3*n + m)*x^n*y^2 + b*(2*n + m)*y - A*x^m - C0/x^n)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 518*)


pde =  (a*x^n + b*x^2 + x*y)*D[w[x, y], x] + (c*x^n + b*x*y + y^2)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 519*)


pde =  (a*y^n + b*x^2 + c*x*y)*D[w[x, y], x] + (k*y^n + b*x*y + c*y^2)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 520*)


pde =  (a*x^n + b*x^m + c)*D[w[x, y], x] + (c*y^2 - b*x^(m - 1)*y + a*x^(n - 2))*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 521*)


pde =  (a*x^n + b*x^m + c)*D[w[x, y], x] + (a*x^(n - 2)*y^2 + b*x^(m - 1)*y + c)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 522*)


pde =  (a*x^n + b*x^m + c)*D[w[x, y], x] + (alpha*x^k*y^2 + beta*x^s*y - alpha*lambda^2*x^k + beta*lambda*x^s)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 523*)


pde =  x*(a*x^n + b*x^m + c)*D[w[x, y], x] - (s*x^k*y^2 - (a*x^n + b*x^m + c)*y - s*lambda*x^(k + 2))*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 524*)


pde =  (a*x^n + b*x^m + c)*D[w[x, y], x] + ((a*x^n + b*x^m + c)*y^2 - a*n*(n - 1)*x^(n - 2) - b*m*(m - 1)*x^(m - 2))*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 525*)


pde =  (a*x^n + b*y^n + x)*D[w[x, y], x] + (alpha*x^k*y^(n - k) + beta*x^m*y^(n - m) + y)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 526*)


pde =  (a*x^n + b*y^n + A*x^2 + B*x*y)*D[w[x, y], x] + (alpha*x^k*y^(n - k) + beta*x^m*y^(n - m) + A*x*y + B*y^2)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 527*)


pde =  (a*y^m + b*x^n + s)*D[w[x, y], x] - (alpha*x^k + b*n*x^(n - 1)*y + beta)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 528*)


pde =  (a*x^n*y^m + x)*D[w[x, y], x] + (b*x^k*y^(n + m - k) + y)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 529*)


pde =  x*(a*x^n*y^m + alpha)*D[w[x, y], x] - y*(b*x^n*y^m + beta)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 530*)


pde =  x*(a*n*x^k*y^(n + k) + s)*D[w[x, y], x] - y*(b*m*x^(m + k)*y^k + s)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 531*)


pde =  (a*x^n*y^m + A*x^2 + B*x*y)*D[w[x, y], x] + (b*x^k*y^(n + m - k) + A*x*y + B*y^2)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 532*)


pde =  (a*x^n*y^m + b*x*y^k)*D[w[x, y], x] + (alpha*y^s + beta)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 533*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + a*Exp[lambda*x]*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 534*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*Exp[lambda*x] + b)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 535*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*Exp[lambda*y] + b)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 536*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*Exp[lambda*y + beta*x] + b)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 537*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*Exp[lambda*y + beta*x] + b*Exp[gamma*x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 538*)


pde =  a*Exp[lambda*x]*D[w[x, y], x] + b*Exp[beta*y]*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 539*)

pde =  (a*Exp[lambda*x] + b)*D[w[x, y], x] + (c + Exp[beta*x] + d)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 540*)

pde =  (a*Exp[lambda*x] + b)*D[w[x, y], x] + (c + Exp[beta*y] + d)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 541*)

pde =  (a*Exp[lambda*y] + b)*D[w[x, y], x] + (c + Exp[beta*x] + d)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 542*)


pde =  (a*Exp[lambda*x] + b*Exp[beta*y])*D[w[x, y], x] + a*lambda*Exp[lambda*x]*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 543*)


pde =  (a*Exp[lambda*x + beta*y] + c*mu)*D[w[x, y], x] - (b*Exp[gamma*x + mu*y] + c*lambda)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 544*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (y^2 + a*lambda*Exp[lambda*x] - a^2*Exp[2*lambda*x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 545*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (y^2 + b*y + a*(lambda - b)*Exp[lambda*x] - a^2*Exp[2*lambda*x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 546*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (y^2 + a*Exp[lambda*x]*y - a*b*Exp[lambda*x] - b^2)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 547*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] - (y^2 - a*x*Exp[lambda*x]*y + a*Exp[lambda*x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 548*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*Exp[lambda*x]*y^2 + b*Exp[-(lambda*x)])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 549*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*Exp[lambda*x]*y^2 + b*mu*Exp[mu*x] - a*b^2*Exp[(lambda + 2*mu)*x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 550*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*Exp[lambda*x]*y^2 + b*y + c*Exp[-(lambda*x)])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 551*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*Exp[lambda*x]*y^2 + mu*y - a*b^2*Exp[(lambda + 2*mu)*x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 552*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (Exp[lambda*x]*y^2 + a*Exp[mu*x]*y + a*lambda*Exp[(mu - lambda)*x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 553*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] - (lambda*Exp[lambda*x]*y^2 - a*Exp[mu*x]*y + a*lambda*Exp[(mu - lambda)*x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 554*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*Exp[lambda*x]*y^2 + a*b*Exp[(lambda + mu)*x]*y - b*mu*Exp[mu*x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 555*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*Exp[(2*lambda + mu)*x]*y^2 + (b*Exp[(lambda + mu)*x] - lambda)*y + c*Exp[mu*x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 556*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (Exp[lambda*x]*(y - b*Exp[mu*x])^2 + b*mu*Exp[mu*x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 557*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*Exp[lambda*x]*y^2 + b*n*x^(n - 1) - a*b^2*Exp[lambda*x]*x^(2*n))*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 558*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (Exp[lambda*x]*y^2 + a*x^n*y + a*lambda*x^n*Exp[-(lambda*x)])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 559*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (lambda*Exp[lambda*x]*y^2 + a*x^n*Exp[lambda*x]*y - a*x^n*Exp[2*lambda*x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 560*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*Exp[lambda*x]*y^2 - a*b*x^n*Exp[lambda*x]*y + b*n*x^(n - 1))*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 561*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*x^n*y^2 + b*lambda*Exp[lambda*x] - a*b^2*x^n*Exp[2*lambda*x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 562*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*x^n*y^2 + lambda*y - a*b^2*x^n*Exp[2*lambda*x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 563*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*x^n*y^2 - a*b*x^n*Exp[lambda*x]*y + b*lambda*Exp[lambda*x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 564*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*x^n*y^2 - a*x^n*(b*Exp[lambda*x] + c)*y + b*lambda*Exp[lambda*x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 565*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*x^n*Exp[2*lambda*x]*y^2 + (b*x^n*Exp[lambda*x] - lambda)*y + c*x^n)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 566*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*Exp[lambda*x]*(y - b*x^n - c)^2 + b*n*x^(n - 1))*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 567*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (y^2 + 2*a*lambda*x*Exp[lambda*x^2] - a^2*Exp[2*lambda*x^2])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 568*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*Exp[-(lambda*x^2)]*y^2 + lambda*x*y + a*b^2)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 569*)

pde = D[w[x, y], x] + (a*x^n*y^2 + lambda*x*y + a*b^2*x^n*Exp[lambda*x^2])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 570*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*Exp[2*lambda*x]*y^3 + b*Exp[lambda*x]*y^2 + c*y + d*Exp[-(lambda*x)])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 571*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*Exp[lambda*x]*y^3 + 3*a*b*Exp[lambda*x]*y^2 + c*y - 2*a*b^3*Exp[lambda*x] + b*c)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 572*)


pde =  x*D[w[x, y], x] + (a*Exp[lambda*x]*y^2 + k*y + a*b^2*x^(2*k)*Exp[lambda*x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 573*)


pde =  x*D[w[x, y], x] + (a*x^(2*n)*Exp[lambda*x]*y^2 + (b*x^n*Exp[lambda*x] - n)*y + c*Exp[lambda*x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 574*)


pde =  y*D[w[x, y], x] + Exp[lambda*x]*((2*a*lambda*x + a + b)*y - Exp[lambda*x]*(a^2*lambda*x^2 + a*b*x - c))*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 575*)


pde =  a*Exp[lambda*x]*D[w[x, y], x] + b*y^m*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 576*)


pde =  (a*Exp[y] + b*x)*D[w[x, y], x] + D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 577*)


pde =  (a*x^n*Exp[lambda*y] + b*x*y^m)*D[w[x, y], x] + Exp[mu*y]*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 578*)


pde =  (a*x^n*y^m + b*x*Exp[lambda*y])*D[w[x, y], x] + y^k*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 579*)


pde =  (a*x^n*y^m + b*x*y^k)*D[w[x, y], x] + Exp[lambda*y]*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 580*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + a*Sinh[lambda*x]*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 581*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + a*Sinh[mu*y]*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 582*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (y^2 - a^2 + a*lambda*Sinh[lambda*x] - a^2*Sinh[lambda*x]^2)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 583*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + lambda*(Sinh[lambda*x]*y^2 - Sinh[lambda*x]^3)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 584*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + ((a*Sinh[lambda*x]^2 - lambda)*y^2 - a*Sinh[lambda*x]^2 + lambda - a)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 585*)

pde =  Sinh[lambda*x]*D[w[x, y], x] + a*Sinh[mu*y]*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 586*)


pde =  Sinh[mu*yx]*D[w[x, y], x] + a*Sinh[lambda*x]*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 587*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + a*Cosh[lambda*x]*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 588*)

pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + a*Cosh[lambda*y]*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 589*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + ((a*Cosh[lambda*x]^2 - lambda)*y^2 - a*Cosh[lambda*x]^2 + lambda + a)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 590*)


pde =  2*D[w[x, y], x] + ((a - lambda + a*Cosh[lambda*x])*y^2 + a + lambda - a*Cosh[lambda*x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 591*)


pde =  (a*x^n + b*x*Cosh[y]^m)*D[w[x, y], x] + y^k*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 592*)


pde =  (a*x^n + b*x*Cosh[y]^m)*D[w[x, y], x] + Cosh[lambda*y]^k*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 593*)


pde =  (a*x^n*y^m + b*x)*D[w[x, y], x] + Cosh[lambda*y]^k*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 594*)


pde =  Cosh[mu*y]*D[w[x, y], x] + a*Cosh[lambda*x]*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 595*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + a*Tanh[lambda*x]*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 596*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + a*Tanh[lambda*y]*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 597*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (y^2 + a*lambda - a*(a + lambda)*Tanh[lambda*x]^2)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 598*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (y^2 + 3*a*lambda - lambda^2 - a*(a + lambda)*Tanh[lambda*x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 599*)


pde =  (a*x^n + b*x*Tanh[y]^m)*D[w[x, y], x] + y^k*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 600*)


pde =  (a*x^n + b*x*Tanh[y]^m)*D[w[x, y], x] + Tanh[lambda*y]^k*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 601*)


pde =  (a*x^n*y^m + b*x)*D[w[x, y], x] + Tanh[lambda*y]^k*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 602*)


pde =  (a*x^n*Tanh[y]^m)*D[w[x, y], x] + y^k*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 603*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + a*Coth[lambda*x]*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 604*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + a*Coth[lambda*y]*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 605*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (y^2 + a*lambda - a*(a + lambda)*Coth[lambda*x]^2)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 606*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (y^2 + 3*a*lambda - lambda^2 - a*(a + lambda)*Coth[lambda*x]^2)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 607*)

pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + a*Sinh[lambda*x]*Cosh[mu*y]*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 608*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + a*Cosh[lambda*x]*Sinh[mu*y]*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 609*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (y^2 - 2*lambda^2*Tanh[lambda*x]^2 - 2*lambda^2*Coth[lambda*x]^2)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 610*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (y^2 + lambda*(a + b) - 2*a*b - a*(a + lambda)*Tanh[lambda*x]^2 - b*(b + lambda)*Coth[lambda*x]^2)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 611*)

pde =  Sinh[lambda*y]*D[w[x, y], x] + a*Cosh[beta*x]*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 612*)


pde =  (a*x^n*Cosh[lambda*y]^m + b*x)*D[w[x, y], x] + Sinh[beta*y]^k*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 613*)

pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*Log[lambda*x]^k + b)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 614*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*Log[lambda*y]^k + b)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 615*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + a*Log[x + lambda*y]^k*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 616*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + a*x^n*Log[lambda*y]^k*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 617*)

pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + a*y^n*Log[lambda*x]^k*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 618*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (y^2 + a*Log[beta*x]*y - a*b*Log[beta*x] - b^2)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 619*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (y^2 + a*x*Log[b*x]^m*y + a*Log[b*x]^m)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 620*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*x^n*y^2 - a*b*x^(n + 1)*y*Log[x] + b*Log[x] + b)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 621*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] - ((n + 1)*x^n*y^2 - a*x^(n + 1)*Log[x]^m*y + a*Log[x]^m)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 622*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*Log[x]^n*y^2 + b*m*x^(m - 1) - a*b^2*x^(2*m)*Log[x]^n)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 623*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*Log[x]^n*y^2 - a*b*x*y*Log[x]^(n + 1) + b*Log[x] + b)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 624*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*Log[x]^k*(y - b*x^n - c)^3 + b*n*x^(n - 1))*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 625*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*Log[x]^n*y^2 + b*Log[x]^m*y + b*c*Log[x]^m - a*c^2*Log[x]^n)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 626*)


pde =  x*D[w[x, y], x] + (a*y + b*Log[x])^2*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 627*)


pde =  x*D[w[x, y], x] + (x*y^2 - A^2*x*Log[beta*x]^2 + A)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 628*)


pde =  x*D[w[x, y], x] + (x*y^2 - A^2*x*Log[beta*x]^(2*k) + k*A*Log[beta*x]^(k - 1))*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 629*)


pde =  x*D[w[x, y], x] + (a*x^n*y^2 + b - a*b^2*x^n*Log[x]^2)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 630*)


pde =  x*D[w[x, y], x] + (a*Log[lambda*x]^m*y^2 + k*y + a*b^2*x^(2*k)*Log[lambda*x]^m)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 631*)

pde =  x*D[w[x, y], x] + (a*x^n*(y + b*Log[x])^2 - b)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 632*)

pde =  x*D[w[x, y], x] + (a*x^(2*n)*Log[x]*y^2 + (b*x^n*Log[x] - n)*y + c*Log[x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 633*)


pde =  x^k*D[w[x, y], x] + (a*y^n*Log[x]^m + b*y*Log[x]^s)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 634*)


pde =  (a*Log[x] + b)*D[w[x, y], x] + (y^2 + c*Log[x]^n*y - lambda^2 + lambda*c*Log[x]^n)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 635*)


pde =  (a*Log[x] + b)*D[w[x, y], x] + (Log[x]^n*y^2 - c*y - lambda^2*Log[x]^n + c*lambda)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 636*)


pde =  x^2*Log[a*x]*D[w[x, y], x] - (x^2*y^2*Log[a*x] + 1)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 637*)


pde =  Log[lambda*x]^k*D[w[x, y], x] + (a*y^n + b*y*Log[x]^m)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 638*)


pde =  Log[lambda*x]^k*D[w[x, y], x] + (a*y^n*Log[x]^m + b*y)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 639*)

pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*Sin[lambda*x]^k + b)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y},Assumptions -> {Element[k, Integers], k > 0}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 640*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*Sin[lambda*y]^k + b)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 641*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + a*Sin[lambda*x]^k*Sin[mu*y]^n*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 642*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + a*Sin[x + lambda*y]^k*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 643*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (y^2 - a^2 + a*lambda*Sin[lambda*x] + a^2*Sin[lambda*x]^2)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 644*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (y^2 + a*Sin[beta*x]*y + a*b*Sin[beta*x] - b^2)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 645*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (y^2 + a*x*Sin[b*x]^m*y + a*Sin[b*x]^m)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 646*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (lambda*Sin[lambda*x]*y^2 + lambda*Sin[lambda*x]^3)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 647*)


pde =  2*D[w[x, y], x] + ((lambda + a - a*Sin[lambda*x])*y^2 + lambda - a - a*Sin[lambda*x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 648*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + ((lambda + a*Sin[lambda*x]^2)*y^2 + lambda - a + a*Sin[lambda*x]^2)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 649*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] - ((k + 1)*x^k*y^2 - a*x^(k + 1)*Sin[x]^m*y + a*Sin[x]^m)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 650*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*Sin[lambda*x + mu]^k*(y - b*x^n - c)^2 + y - b*x^n + b*n*x^(n - 1) - c)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 651*)


pde =  x*D[w[x, y], x] + (a*Sin[lambda*x]^m*y^2 + k*y + a*b^2*x^(2*k)*Sin[lambda*x]^m)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 652*)


pde =  (a*Sin[lambda*x] + b)*D[w[x, y], x] + (y^2 + c*Sin[mu*x]*y - k^2 + c*k*Sin[mu*x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)

pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*Cos[lambda*x]^k + b)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 654*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*Cos[lambda*y]^k + b)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 655*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + a*Cos[lambda*y]^k*Cos[mu*y]^n*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 656*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + a*Cos[x + lambda*y]^k*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 657*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (y^2 - a^2 + a*lambda*Cos[lambda*x] + a^2*Cos[lambda*x]^2)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 658*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (lambda*Cos[lambda*x]*y^2 + lambda*Cos[lambda*x]^3)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 659*)


pde =  2*D[w[x, y], x] + ((lambda + a + a*Cos[lambda*x])*y^2 + lambda - a + a*Cos[lambda*x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 660*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + ((lambda + a*Cos[lambda*x]^2)*y^2 + lambda - a + a*Cos[lambda*x]^2)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 661*)


pde =  (a*x^n*y^m + b*x)*D[w[x, y], x] + Cos[lambda*y]^k*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 662*)


pde =  (a*x^n + b*x*Cos[y]^m)*D[w[x, y], x] + y^k*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 663*)


pde =  (a*x^n + b*x*Cos[y]^m)*D[w[x, y], x] + Cos[lambda*y]^k*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 664*)


pde =  (a*x^n*Cos[y]^m + b*x)*D[w[x, y], x] + Cos[lambda*y]^k*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 665*)

pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a + Tan[lambda*x] + b)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 666*)

pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a + Tan[lambda*y] + b)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 667*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + a*Tan[lambda*x]^k*Tan[mu*y]^n*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 668*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (y^2 + a*lambda + a*(lambda - a)*Tan[lambda*x]^2)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 669*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (y^2 + lambda^2 + 3*a*lambda + a*(lambda - a)*Tan[lambda*x]^2)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 670*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (y^2 + a*x*Tan[b*x]^k*y + a*Tan[b*x]^k)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 671*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] - ((k + 1)*x^k*y^2 - a*x^(k + 1)*Tan[x]^m*y + a*Tan[x]^m)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 672*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*Tan[lambda*x]^n*y^2 - a*b^2*Tan[lambda*x]^(n + 2) + b*lambda*Tan[lambda*x]^2 + b*lambda)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 673*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*Tan[lambda*x + mu]^k*(y - b*x^n - c)^2 + y - b*x^n + b*n*x^(n - 1) - c)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 674*)


pde =  x*D[w[x, y], x] + (a*Tan[lambda*x]^m*y^2 + k*y + a*b^2*x^(2*k)*Tan[lambda*x]^m)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 675*)

sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 676*)

sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 681*)

sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 682*)

sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 683*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (y^2 + a*lambda + a*(lambda - a)*Cot[lambda*x]^2)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 684*)

sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 685*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (y^2 - 2*a*Cot[a*x]*y + b^2 - a^2)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 686*)

pde =  Cot[lambda*x]*D[w[x, y], x] + a*Cot[mu*y]*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 687*)

pde =  Cot[mu*y]*D[w[x, y], x] + a*Cot[lambda*x]*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 688*)

pde =  Cot[mu*y]*D[w[x, y], x] + a*Cot[lambda*x]^2*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 689*)


pde =  Cot[y + a]*D[w[x, y], x] + c*Cot[x + b]^2*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 690*)

pde =  Cot[lambda*x]*Cot[mu*y]*D[w[x, y], x] + a*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 691*)


pde =  Cot[lambda*x]*Cot[mu*y]*D[w[x, y], x] + a*Cot[v*x]*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 692*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + a*Sin[lambda*x]^k*Cos[mu*y]^n*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 693*)

pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (y^2 - y*Tan[x] + a*(1 - a)*Cot[x]^2)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 694*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (y^2 - m*y*Tan[x] + b^2*Cos[x]^(2*m))*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 695*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (y^2 + m*y*Cot[x] + b^2*Sin[x]^m)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 696*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (y^2 - 2*lambda^2*Tan[lambda*x]^2 - 2*lambda^2*Cot[lambda*x]^2)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 697*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (y^2 + lambda*(a + b) + 2*a*b + a*(lambda - a)*Tan[lambda*x]^2 + b*(lambda - b)*Cot[lambda*x]^2)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 698*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (lambda*Sin[lambda*x]*y^2 + a*Cos[lambda*x]^n*y - a*Cos[lambda*x]^(n - 1))*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 699*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (lambda*Sin[lambda*x]*y^2 + a*Sin[lambda*x]*y - a*Tan[lambda*x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 700*)

sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 701*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (A*Exp[lambda*x]*Cos[a*y] + B*Exp[mu*x]*Sin[a*y] + A*Exp[lambda*x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 702*)


pde =  Sin[2*x]^(n + 1)*D[w[x, y], x] + (a*y^2*Sin[x]^(2*n) + b*Cos[x]^(2*n))*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;

(*----------------------------*)

(*----------------------------*)

sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)

sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*PDE number 743*)

(*PDE number 745*)

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 746*)


pde = D[w[x, y], x] + (lambda*ArcCot[x]^n*y^2 + a*y + a*b - b^2*lambda*ArcCot[x]^n*n)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;

(*----------------------------*)

pde = D[w[x, y], x] + (lambda*ArcCot[x]^n*y^2 - b*lambda*x^m*ArcCot[x]^n*y + b*m*x^(m - 1))*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol = AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)

pde = D[w[x, y], x] + (lambda*ArcCot[x]^n*y^2 + b*m*x^(m - 1) - lambda*b^2*x^(2*m)*ArcCot[x]^n)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol = AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

sol = AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 750*)


pde = x*D[w[x, y], x] + (lambda*ArcCot[x]^n*y^2 + k*y + lambda*b^2*x^(2*k)*ArcCot[x]^n)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol = AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 751*)

sol = AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*PDE number 756*)

sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 757*)
