sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 758*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (f[x]*y^2 + a*n*x^(n - 1) - a^2*x^(2*n)*f[x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 759*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (f[x]*y^2 + g[x]*y - a^2*f[x] - a*g[x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 760*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (f[x]*y^2 + g[x]*y + a*n*x^(n - 1) - a*x^n*g[x] - a^2*x^(2*n)*f[x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 761*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (f[x]*y^2 - a*x^n*g*x*y + a*n*x^(n - 1) + a^2*x^(2*n)*(g*x - f*x))*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 762*)


pde =  x*D[w[x, y], x] + (f[x]*y^2 + n*y + a*x^(2*n)*f[x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}, Assumptions -> a > 0], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 763*)


pde =  x*D[w[x, y], x] + (x^(2*n)*f[x]*y^2 + (a*x^n*f[x] - n)*y + b*f[x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 764*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*Exp[lambda*x]*y^2 + a*Exp[lambda*x]*f[x]*y + lambda*f[x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 765*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (f[x]*y^2 - a*Exp[lambda*x]*f[x]*y + a*lambda*Exp[lambda*x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 766*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (f[x]*y^2 + a*lambda*Exp[lambda*x] - a^2*Exp[2*lambda*x]*f[x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 767*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (f[x]*y^2 + lambda*y + a*Exp[2*lambda*x]*f[x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}, Assumptions -> a > 0], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 768*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (f[x]*y^2 - (a*Exp[lambda*x] + b)*f[x]*y + a*lambda*Exp[lambda*x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 769*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (Exp[lambda*x]*f[x]*y^2 + (a*f[x] - lambda)*y + b*Exp[-(lambda*x)]*f[x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 770*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (f[x]*y^2 + g[x]*y + a*lambda*Exp[lambda*x] - a*Exp[lambda*x]*g[x] - a^2*Exp[2*lambda*x]*f[x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 771*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (f[x]*y^2 - a*Exp[lambda*x]*g[x]*y + a*lambda*Exp[lambda*x] + a^2*Exp[2*lambda*x]*(g[x] - f[x]))*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 772*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (f[x]*y^2 + 2*a*lambda*x*Exp[lambda*x^2] - a^2*f[x]*Exp[2*lambda*x^2])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 773*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (f[x]*y^2 + 2*lambda*x*y + a*f[x]*Exp[2*lambda*x^2])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}, Assumptions -> a > 0], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 774*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (f[x]*Exp[lambda*y] + g[x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 775*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (f[x]*y^2 - a^2*f[x] + a*lambda*Sinh[lambda*x] - a^2*f[x]*Sinh[lambda*x]^2)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 776*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (f[x]*y^2 - a*(a*f[x] + lambda)*Tanh[lambda*x]^2 + a*lambda)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 777*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (f[x]*y^2 - a*(a*f[x] + lambda)*Coth[lambda*x]^2 + a*lambda)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 778*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] - (a*y^2*Log[x] - a*x*y*(Log[x] - 1)*f[x] + f[x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 779*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (f[x]*y^2 - a*x*Log[x]*f[x]*y + a*Log[x] + a)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 780*)


pde =  x*D[w[x, y], x] + (f[x]*y^2 + a - a^2*Log[x]^2*f[x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 781*)


pde =  x*D[w[x, y], x] + ((y + a*Log[x])^2*f[x] - a)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 782*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (lambda*Sin[lambda*x]*y^2 + f[x]*Cos[lambda*x]*y - f[x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 783*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (f[x]*y^2 - a^2*f[x] + a*lambda*Sin[lambda*x] + a^2*f[x]*Sin[lambda*x]^2)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 784*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (f[x]*y^2 - a^2*f[x] + a*lambda*Cos[lambda*x] + a^2*f[x]*Cos[lambda*x]^2)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 785*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (f[x]*y^2 - a*(a*f[x] - lambda)*Tan[lambda*x]^2 + a*lambda)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 786*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (f[x]*y^2 - a*(a*f[x] - lambda)*Cot[lambda*x]^2 + a*lambda)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 787*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (f[x]*y^2 - f[x]*g[x]*y + Derivative[1][g][x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 788*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] - (Derivative[1][f][x]*y^2 - f[x]*g[x]*y + g[x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 789*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (g[x]*(y - f[x])^2 + Derivative[1][f][x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 790*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + ((Derivative[1][f][x]*y^2)/g[x] - Derivative[1][g][x]/f[x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 791*)


pde =  f[x]^2*D[w[x, y], x] + (Derivative[1][f][x]*y^2 - g[x]*(y - f[x]))*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 792*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (y^2 - Derivative[2][f][x]/f[x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 793*)


pde =  g[x]*D[w[x, y], x] + (a*f[x]*g[x]*y^3 + (b*f[x]*g[x]^3 + Derivative[1][g][x])*y + c*f[x]*g[x]^4)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 794*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (f[x]*y^3 + 3*f[x]*h[x]*y^2 + (g[x] + 3*f[x]*h[x]^2)*y + f[x]*h[x]^3 + g[x]*h[x] - Derivative[1][h][x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 795*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + ((Derivative[1][g][x]*y^3)/(f[x]^2*(a*g[x] + b)^3) + (Derivative[1][f][x]*y)/f[x] + f[x]*Derivative[1][g][x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 796*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + ((y - f[x])*(y - g[x])*(y - (a*f[x] + b*g[x])/(a + b))*h[x] + ((y - g[x])*Derivative[1][f][x])/(f[x] - g[x]) + ((y - f[x])*Derivative[1][g][x])/(g[x] - f[x]))*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 797*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (f[x]*y^2 + Derivative[1][g][x]*y + a*f[x]*Exp[2*g[x]])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}, Assumptions -> a > 0], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 798*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (Derivative[1][f][x]*y^2 + a*Exp[lambda*x]*f[x]*y + a*Exp[lambda*x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 799*)


pde =  f[x]*D[w[x, y], x] + g[y]*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 800*)


pde =  (f[x] + g[y])*D[w[x, y], x] + Derivative[1][f][x]*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 801*)


pde =  (x^n*f[y] + x*g[y])*D[w[x, y], x] + h[y]*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 802*)


pde =  (f[y] + a*m*x^n*y^(m - 1))*D[w[x, y], x] - (g[x] + a*n*x^(n - 1)*y^m)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 803*)


pde =  (Exp[alpha*x]*f[y] + c*beta)*D[w[x, y], x] - (Exp[beta*y]*g[x] + c*alpha)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 804*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + f[a*x + b*y + c]*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 805*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + f[y/x]*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 806*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (f[y + a*x^n + b] - a*n*x^(n - 1))*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 807*)


pde =  x*D[w[x, y], x] + y*f[x^n*y^m]*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 808*)


pde =  y^(m - 1)*D[w[x, y], x] + x^(n - 1)*f[a*x^n + b*y^m]*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 809*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + Exp[-(lambda*x)]*f[Exp[lambda*x]*y]*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 810*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + Exp[lambda*y]*f[Exp[lambda*y]*x]*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 811*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + y*f[Exp[alpha*x]*y^m]*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 812*)


pde =  x*D[w[x, y], x] + f[x^n*Exp[alpha*y]]*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 813*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + Exp[lambda*x - beta*y]*f[a*Exp[lambda*x] + b*Exp[beta*y]]*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 814*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (f[y + a*Exp[lambda*x] + b] - a*lambda*Exp[lambda*x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 815*)


pde =  alpha*x*y*D[w[x, y], x] + (alpha*f[x^n*Exp[alpha*y]] - n*y)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 816*)


pde =  m*x*Log[y]*D[w[x, y], x] + (y*f[x^n*y^m] - n*y*Log[y])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 817*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (f[y + a*Tan[x]] - a*Tan[x]^2)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 818*)


pde =  Exp[lambda*x]*D[w[x, y], x] + f[lambda*x + Log[y]]*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 819*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + Exp[lambda*y]*f[lambda*y + Log[x]]*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 820*)


pde =  m*x*D[w[x, y], x] - (n*y - x*y^k*f[x]*g[x^n*y^m])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 821*)


pde =  y^n*D[w[x, y], x] - (a*x^n + g[x]*f[y^(n + 1) + a*x^(n + 1)])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 822*)


pde =  (f[y/x] + x^alpha*h[y/x])*D[w[x, y], x] + (g[y/x] + y*x^(alpha - 1)*h[y/x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 823*)


pde =  (f[a*x + b*y] + b*x*g[a*x + b*y])*D[w[x, y], x] + (h[a*x + b*y] - a*x*g[a*x + b*y])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 824*)


pde =  (f[a*x + b*y] + b*y*g[a*x + b*y])*D[w[x, y], x] + (h[a*x + b*y] - a*y*g[a*x + b*y])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 825*)


pde =  x*(f[x^n*y^m] + m*x^k*g[x^n*y^m])*D[w[x, y], x] + y*(h[x^n*y^m] - n*x^k*g[x^n*y^m])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 826*)


pde =  x*(f[x^n*y^m] + m*y^k*g[x^n*y^m])*D[w[x, y], x] + y*(h[x^n*y^m] - n*y^k*g[x^n*y^m])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 827*)


pde =  x*(s*f[x^n*y^m] - m*g[x^k*y^s])*D[w[x, y], x] + y*(n*g[x^k*y^s] - k*f[x^n*y^m])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 828*)


pde =  D[f[x, y], y]*D[w[x, y], x] - D[f[x, y], x]*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 829*)


pde =  x*D[w[x, y], x] + (x*f[x]*g[x^n*Exp[y]] - n)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 830*)


pde =  m*D[w[x, y], x] + (m*y^k*f[x]*g[Exp[alpha*x]*y^m] - alpha*y)*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 831*)


pde =  (f[a*x + b*y] + b*Exp[lambda*y]*g[a*x + b*y])*D[w[x, y], x] + (h[a*x + b*y] - a*Exp[lambda*y]*g[a*x + b*y])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 832*)


pde =  (f[a*x + b*y] + b*Exp[lambda*x]*g[a*x + b*y])*D[w[x, y], x] + (h[a*x + b*y] - a*Exp[lambda*x]*g[a*x + b*y])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 833*)


pde =  x*(f[x^n*Exp[alpha*y]] + alpha*y*g[x^n*Exp[alpha*y]])*D[w[x, y], x] + (h[x^n*Exp[alpha*y]] - n*y*g[x^n*Exp[alpha*y]])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 834*)


pde =  (f[Exp[alpha*x]*y^m] + m*x*g[Exp[alpha*x]*y^m])*D[w[x, y], x] + y*(h[Exp[alpha*x]*y^m] - alpha*x*g[Exp[alpha*x]*y^m])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 835*)


pde =  x*D[w[x, y], x] + (x*y*f[x]*g[x^n*Log[y]] - n*y*Log[y])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 836*)


pde =  x*(f[x^n*y^m] + m*g[x^n*y^m]*Log[y])*D[w[x, y], x] + y*(h[x^n*y^m] - n*g[x^n*y^m]*Log[y])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 837*)


pde =  x*(f[x^n*y^m] + m*g[x^n*y^m]*Log[x])*D[w[x, y], x] + y*(h[x^n*y^m] - n*g[x^n*y^m]*Log[x])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 838*)


pde =  Cos[y]*D[w[x, y], x] + (f[x]*g[Sin[x]*Sin[y]] - Cot[x]*Sin[y])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 839*)


pde =  Sin[2*x]*D[w[x, y], x] + (Sin[2*x]*Cos[y]^2*f[x]*g[Tan[x]*Tan[y]] - Sin[2*y])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 840*)


pde =  x*D[w[x, y], x] + (x*Cos[y]^2*f[x]*g[x^(2*n)*Tan[y]] - n*Sin[2*y])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 841*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (Cos[y]^2*f[x]*g[Exp[2*x]*Tan[y]] - Sin[2*y])*D[w[x, y], y] == 0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 842*)


pde =  a*x*D[w[x, y], x] + b*y*D[w[x, y], y] == c;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 843*)


pde =  a*Exp[x]*D[w[x, y], x] + b*D[w[x, y], y] == c*Exp[2*x]*y;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 844*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + a*D[w[x, y], y] == b;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 845*)


pde =  a*D[w[x, y], x] + b*D[w[x, y], y] == c;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 846*)


pde =  a*D[w[x, y], x] + b*D[w[x, y], y] == alpha*x + beta*y + gamma;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 847*)


pde =  a*x*D[w[x, y], x] + b*D[w[x, y], y] == alpha*x + beta*y + gamma;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 848*)


pde =  a*x*D[w[x, y], x] + b*x*D[w[x, y], y] == c;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 849*)


pde =  (a*x + b)*D[w[x, y], x] + (c*y + d)*D[w[x, y], y] == alpha*x + beta*y + gamma;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 850*)


pde =  a*y*D[w[x, y], x] + b*D[w[x, y], y] == alpha*x + beta*y + gamma;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 851*)


pde =  a*y*D[w[x, y], x] + b*x*D[w[x, y], y] == c;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 852*)


pde =  a*y*D[w[x, y], x] + b*x*D[w[x, y], y] == c*x + k*y;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 853*)


pde =  a*D[w[x, y], x] + b*D[w[x, y], y] == c*x^2 + d*y^2 + k*x*y + n;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 854*)


pde =  a*x*D[w[x, y], x] + b*y*D[w[x, y], y] == c*x^2 + d*y^2 + k*x*y + n;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 855*)


pde =  a*y*D[w[x, y], x] + b*x*D[w[x, y], y] == c*x*y + d;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 856*)


pde =  a*x^2*D[w[x, y], x] + b*y^2*D[w[x, y], y] == c*x^2 + d*y^2 + k*x*y + n*x + m*y + s;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 857*)


pde =  x^2*D[w[x, y], x] + a*x*y*D[w[x, y], y] == b*y^2;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 858*)


pde =  a*y^2*D[w[x, y], x] + b*x^2*D[w[x, y], y] == c*x^2 + d;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 859*)


pde =  a*y^2*D[w[x, y], x] + b*x*y*D[w[x, y], y] == c*x^2 + d*y^2;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 860*)


pde =  x*D[w[x, y], x] + y*D[w[x, y], y] == a*Sqrt[x^2 + y^2];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 861*)


pde =  a*x*D[w[x, y], x] + b*y*D[w[x, y], y] == c*x*y^2 + d*x^2*y + k;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 862*)


pde =  a*y*D[w[x, y], x] + b*x*D[w[x, y], y] == c*x*y^2 + d;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 863*)


pde =  (a*x + b)*D[w[x, y], x] + (c*y + d)*D[w[x, y], y] == k*x^3 + n*y^3;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 864*)


pde =  x^2*D[w[x, y], x] + x*y*D[w[x, y], y] == y^2*(a*x + b*y);
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 865*)

pde =  a*x^3*D[w[x, y], x] + b*y^3*D[w[x, y], y] == c*x + d;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 866*)


pde =  a*D[w[x, y], x] + b*D[w[x, y], y] == c*x^n + d*y^m;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 867*)

pde =  a*D[w[x, y], x] + b*D[w[x, y], y] == c*x^n*y;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 868*)


pde =  x*D[w[x, y], x] + y*D[w[x, y], y] == a*(x^2 + y^2)^k;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 869*)


pde =  a*x*D[w[x, y], x] + b*y*D[w[x, y], y] == c*x^n*y^m;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 870*)


pde =  a*x*D[w[x, y], x] + b*y*D[w[x, y], y] == c*x^n + d*y^m;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 871*)


pde =  m*x*D[w[x, y], x] + n*y*D[w[x, y], y] == (a*x^n + b*y^m)^k;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 872*)


pde =  a*x^n*D[w[x, y], x] + b*y^m*D[w[x, y], y] == c*x^k + d*y^s;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 873*)


pde =  a*x^n*D[w[x, y], x] + b*x^m*y*D[w[x, y], y] == c*x^k*y^s + d;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 874*)


pde =  a*x^n*D[w[x, y], x] + (b*x^m*y + c*x^k)*D[w[x, y], y] == s*x^p*y^q + d;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 875*)


pde =  a*x^n*D[w[x, y], x] + (b*x^m*y^k + c*x^r*y)*D[w[x, y], y] == s*x^p*y^q + d;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 876*)


pde =  a*y^k*D[w[x, y], x] + b*x^m*D[w[x, y], y] == c*x^m + d;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 877*)


pde =  a*D[w[x, y], x] + b*D[w[x, y], y] == c*Exp[lambda*x] + d*Exp[mu*y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 878*)


pde =  a*D[w[x, y], x] + b*D[w[x, y], y] == c*Exp[lambda*x + beta*y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 879*)


pde =  a*Exp[lambda*x]*D[w[x, y], x] + b*Exp[beta*y]*D[w[x, y], y] == c;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 880*)


pde =  a*Exp[lambda*y]*D[w[x, y], x] + b*Exp[beta*x]*D[w[x, y], y] == c;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 881*)


pde =  a*Exp[alpha*x]*D[w[x, y], x] + b*Exp[beta*y]*D[w[x, y], y] == c*Exp[gamma*x - beta*y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 882*)


pde =  a*Exp[alpha*x]*D[w[x, y], x] + b*Exp[beta*y]*D[w[x, y], y] == c*Exp[gamma*x - 2*beta*y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 883*)


pde =  a*Exp[alpha*x]*D[w[x, y], x] + b*Exp[beta*y]*D[w[x, y], y] == c*Exp[gamma*x] + s*Exp[mu*y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 884*)


pde =  a*Exp[beta*x]*D[w[x, y], x] + (b*Exp[gamma*x] + c*Exp[lambda*y])*D[w[x, y], y] == s*Exp[mu*x] + k*Exp[delta*y] + p;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 885*)


pde =  a*Exp[beta*x]*D[w[x, y], x] + (b*Exp[gamma*x] + c*Exp[lambda*y])*D[w[x, y], y] == s*Exp[mu*x + delta*y] + k;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 886*)


pde =  a*Exp[beta*x]*D[w[x, y], x] + b*Exp[gamma*x + lambda*y]*D[w[x, y], y] == c*Exp[mu*x + delta*y] + k;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 887*)


pde =  a*Exp[lambda*y]*D[w[x, y], x] + b*Exp[beta*x]*D[w[x, y], y] == c*Exp[gamma*y] + d;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 888*)


pde =  a*D[w[x, y], x] + b*D[w[x, y], y] == c*y*Exp[lambda*x] + k*x*Exp[mu*y];

sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 890*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*y + b*Exp[lambda*x])*D[w[x, y], y] == c*Exp[beta*x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 891*)

sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 892*)

sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)

sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)

(*----------------------------*)

(*----------------------------*)

sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 901*)

sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 902*)

sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)

sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 906*)

(*----------------------------*)

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 909*)

sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 910*)

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 991*)


pde =  f[x]*D[w[x, y], x] + (y + a*x)*D[w[x, y], y] == g[x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 992*)


 pde =  f[x]*D[w[x, y], x] + (y*g1[x] + g0[x])*D[w[x, y], y] == y^2*h2[x] + y*h1[x] + h0[x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];
 

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 993*)


pde =  f[x]*D[w[x, y], x] + (y*g1[x] + y^k*g2[x])*D[w[x, y], y] == h[x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];
 

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 994*)


pde =  f[x]*D[w[x, y], x] + (g1[x] + Exp[lambda*y])*D[w[x, y], y] == h[x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 995*)


pde =  y^k*f[x]*D[w[x, y], x] + g[x]*D[w[x, y], y] == h[x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 996*)


pde =  y^k*f[x]*D[w[x, y], x] + (y^(k + 1)*g1[x] + g0[x])*D[w[x, y], y] == y^(3*k + 1)*h2[x] + y^(2*k + 1)*h1[x] + y^k*h0[x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 997*)


pde =  f[x]*Exp[lambda*x]*D[w[x, y], x] + g[x]*D[w[x, y], y] == h[x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 998*)


pde =  a*D[w[x, y], x] + b*D[w[x, y], y] == f[x] + g[y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 999*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + a*D[w[x, y], y] == f[x]*g[y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1000*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + (a*y + f[x])*D[w[x, y], y] == g[x]*h[y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1001*)


pde =  f[x]*D[w[x, y], x] + g[y]*D[w[x, y], y] == h1[x] + h2[x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1002*)


pde = f1[x]*D[w[x, y], x] + (y*f2[x] + y^k*f3[x])*D[w[x, y], y] == g[x]*h[x];
sol = AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1003*)


pde = f1[x]*g1[x]*D[w[x, y], x] + f2[x]*g2[x]*D[w[x, y], y] == h1[x]*h2[x];
sol = AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1004*)


pde = f1[x]*g1[y]*D[w[x, y], x] + f2[x]*g2[y]*D[w[x, y], y] == h1[x] + h2[x];
sol = AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1005*)


pde = a*D[w[x, y], x] + b*D[w[x, y], y] == f[alpha*x + beta*y];
sol = AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1006*)


pde = x*D[w[x, y], x] + y*D[w[x, y], y] == x*f[y/x];
sol = AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1007*)


pde = x*D[w[x, y], x] + y*D[w[x, y], y] == f[x^2 + y^2];
sol = AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1008*)


pde = x*D[w[x, y], x] + y*D[w[x, y], y] == x*f[y/x] + g[x^2 + y^2];
sol = AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1009*)


pde = a*x*D[w[x, y], x] + b*y*D[w[x, y], y] == x^k*f[x^n*x^m];
sol = AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1010*)


pde = m*x*D[w[x, y], x] + n*y*D[w[x, y], y] == f[a*x^n + b*x^m];
sol = AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1011*)


 
pde =  x^2*D[w[x, y], x] + x*y*D[w[x, y], y] == y^k*f[alpha*x + beta*y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1012*)

pde = (f[x]*D[w[x, y], x])/Derivative[1][f][x] + (g[y]*D[w[x, y], y])/Derivative[1][g][y] == h[f[x] + g[y]];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1013*)


pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + a*D[w[x, y], y] == f[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1014*)


pde =  a*x*D[w[x, y], x] + b*y*D[w[x, y], y] == f[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1015*)


pde =  f[x]*D[w[x, y], x] + g[x]*y*D[w[x, y], y] == h[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1016*)


pde =  f[x]*D[w[x, y], x] + (g1[x]*y + g0[x])*D[w[x, y], y] == h[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1017*)


pde =  f[x]*D[w[x, y], x] + (g1[x]*y + g0[x]*y^k)*D[w[x, y], y] == h[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1018*)


 
 
pde =  f[x]*D[w[x, y], x] + (g1[x] + g0[x]*Exp[lambda*y])*D[w[x, y], y] == h[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1019*)


 
 
pde =  f1[x]*g1[y]*D[w[x, y], x] + f2[x]*g2[y]*D[w[x, y], y] == h[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1020*)


 
 
pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + a*y*D[w[x, y], y] == b*y^2*w[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1021*)


 
 
pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + a*y*D[w[x, y], y] == b*Exp[lambda*x]*y*w[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1022*)


 
 
pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + a*D[w[x, y], y] == b*w[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1023*)


 
 
pde =  a*D[w[x, y], x] + b*D[w[x, y], y] == c*w[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1024*)


 
 
pde =  a*D[w[x, y], x] + y*D[w[x, y], y] == b*w[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1025*)


 
 
pde =  x*D[w[x, y], x] + y*D[w[x, y], y] == a*w[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1026*)


 
 
pde =  x*(D[w[x, y], x] - b*D[w[x, y], y]) == c*y*w[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1027*)


 
 
pde =  x*D[w[x, y], x] + y*D[w[x, y], y] == a*x*w[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1028*)


pde =  (x - a)*D[w[x, y], x] + (y - b)*D[w[x, y], y] == w[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1029*)


pde =  (y + a*x)*D[w[x, y], x] + (y - a*x)*D[w[x, y], y] == b*w[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1030*)

pde =  a*D[w[x, y], x] + b*D[w[x, y], y] == (x^2 - y^2)*w[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1031*)


pde =  x^2*D[w[x, y], x] + a*x*y*D[w[x, y], y] == b*y^2*w[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1032*)


pde =  a*x^2*D[w[x, y], x] + b*y^2*D[w[x, y], y] == (x + c*y)*w[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1033*)


pde =  x^2*D[w[x, y], x] + a*y^2*D[w[x, y], y] == (b*x^2 + c*x*y + d*y^2)*w[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1034*)


pde =  y^2*D[w[x, y], x] + a*x^2*D[w[x, y], y] == (b*x^2 + c*y^2)*w[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1035*)


pde =  x*y*D[w[x, y], x] + a*y^2*D[w[x, y], y] == (b*x + c*y + d)*w[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1036*)


pde =  x*(a*y + b)*D[w[x, y], x] + (a*y^2 - b*x)*D[w[x, y], y] == a*y*w[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1037*)


pde =  x*(k*y - x + a)*D[w[x, y], x] - y*(k*x - y + a)*D[w[x, y], y] == b*(y - x)*w[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1038*)


pde =  a*D[w[x, y], x] + b*D[w[x, y], y] == (c*x^3 + d*y^3)*w[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1039*)


pde =  x*D[w[x, y], x] + y*D[w[x, y], y] == a*Sqrt[x^2 + y^2]*w[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1040*)


pde =  x^2*D[w[x, y], x] + x*y*D[w[x, y], y] == y^2*(a*x + b*y)*w[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1041*)


pde =  x^2*y*D[w[x, y], x] + a*x*y^2*D[w[x, y], y] == (b*x*y + c*x + d*y + k)*w[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1042*)


pde =  a*x*y^2*D[w[x, y], x] + b*x^2*y*D[w[x, y], y] == (a*n*y^2 + b*m*x^2)*w[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1043*)


pde =  x^3*D[w[x, y], x] + a*y^3*D[w[x, y], y] == x^2*(b*x + c*y)*w[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1044*)


pde =  a*D[w[x, y], x] + b*D[w[x, y], y] == (c*x^n + d*y^m)*w[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1045*)


pde =  a*D[w[x, y], x] + b*D[w[x, y], y] == c*x^n*y*w[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];
sol[[2]] = Assuming[{n != -1, n != -2}, Simplify[sol[[2]]]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1046*)


pde =  a*D[w[x, y], x] + b*D[w[x, y], y] == c*x^n*y*w[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}, Assumptions -> n == -1], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1047*)


pde =  a*D[w[x, y], x] + b*D[w[x, y], y] == c*x^n*y*w[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}, Assumptions -> n == -2], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1048*)


pde =  x*D[w[x, y], x] + y*D[w[x, y], y] == a*(x^2 + y^2)^k*w[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1049*)


pde =  a*x*D[w[x, y], x] + b*y*D[w[x, y], y] == c*x^n*y^m*w[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1050*)


pde =  a*x*D[w[x, y], x] + b*y*D[w[x, y], y] == (c*x^n + k*y^m)*w[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1051*)


pde =  m*x*D[w[x, y], x] + n*y*D[w[x, y], y] == (a*x^n + b*y^m)^k*w[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1052*)


pde =  a*x^n*D[w[x, y], x] + b*y^m*D[w[x, y], y] == (c*x^k + d*y^s)*w[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1053*)


pde =  a*x^n*D[w[x, y], x] + b*x^m*y*D[w[x, y], y] == (c*x^k*y^s + d)*w[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1054*)


pde =  a*x^n*D[w[x, y], x] + (b*x^m*y + c*x^k)*D[w[x, y], y] == (s*x^p*y^q + d)*w[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1055*)


pde =  a*x^n*D[w[x, y], x] + b*x^m*y^k*D[w[x, y], y] == (c*x^p*y^q + s)*w[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1056*)


pde =  a*y^k*D[w[x, y], x] + b*x^n*D[w[x, y], y] == (c*x^m + s)*w[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1057*)


pde =  x*(x^n + (a*n - 1)*y^n)*D[w[x, y], x] + y*(y^n + (a*n - 1)*x^n)*D[w[x, y], y] == k*n*(x^n + y^n)*w[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1058*)


pde = x*((n - 2)*y^n - 2*x^n)*D[w[x, y], x] + y*(2*y^n - (n - 2)*x^n)*D[w[x, y], y] == ((a*(n - 2) + 2*b)*y^n - (2*a + b*(n - 2))*x^n)*w[x, y];
sol = AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1059*)


pde = a*D[w[x, y], x] + b*D[w[x, y], y] == c*Exp[alpha*x + beta*y]*w[x, y];
sol = AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1060*)


pde = a*D[w[x, y], x] + b*D[w[x, y], y] == (c*Exp[lambda*x] + k*Exp[mu*y])*w[x, y];
sol = AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1061*)


pde = a*Exp[lambda*x]*D[w[x, y], x] + b*Exp[beta*y]*D[w[x, y], y] == c*w[x, y];
sol = AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1062*)


 
 
pde =  a*Exp[lambda*y]*D[w[x, y], x] + b*Exp[beta*x]*D[w[x, y], y] == c*w[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1063*)


 
 
pde =  a*Exp[lambda*x]*D[w[x, y], x] + b*Exp[beta*x]*D[w[x, y], y] == c*Exp[gamma*y]*w[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1064*)


pde =  a*Exp[lambda*x]*D[w[x, y], x] + b*Exp[beta*y]*D[w[x, y], y] == (c*Exp[gamma*y] + s*Exp[delta*y])*w[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1065*)


pde =  a*Exp[beta*x]*D[w[x, y], x] + (b*Exp[gamma*x] + c*Exp[lambda*y])*D[w[x, y], y] == (s*Exp[mu*x] + k*Exp[delta*y] + p)*w[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1066*)


pde =  a*Exp[beta*x]*D[w[x, y], x] + (b*Exp[gamma*x] + c*Exp[lambda*y])*D[w[x, y], y] == (s*Exp[mu*x + delta*y] + k)*w[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1067*)


pde =  a*Exp[beta*x]*D[w[x, y], x] + (b*Exp[gamma*x + lambda*y])*D[w[x, y], y] == (c*Exp[mu*x + delta*y] + k)*w[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1068*)


pde =  a*Exp[lambda*x]*D[w[x, y], x] + b*Exp[beta*x]*D[w[x, y], y] == (c*Exp[mu*x] + k)*w[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1069*)

(*----------------------------*)

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1183*)

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1185*)


pde =  a*x*D[w[x, y], x] + b*y*D[w[x, y], y] == x^k*f[x^n*y^m]*w[x, y];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1186*)

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1187*)

sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1188*)


 
 
pde =  (f[x]*D[w[x, y], x])/Derivative[1][f][x] + (g[x]*D[w[x, y], y])/Derivative[1][g][x] == h[f[x] + g[y]]*w[x, y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1189*)


 
 
pde =  D[w[x, y], x] + a*D[w[x, y], y] == f[x, y]*w[x, y];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1190*)


 
 
pde =  a*x*D[w[x, y], x] + b*y*D[w[x, y], y] == f[x, y]*w[x, y];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1191*)

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1192*)

(*PDE number 1195*)

sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1196*)

pde =  a*D[w[x, y], x] + b*D[w[x, y], y] == c*w[x, y] + d;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1197*)


 
 
pde =  (x - a)*D[w[x, y], x] + (y - b)*D[w[x, y], y] == w[x, y] - c;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1198*)

pde =  (a*x + b)*D[w[x, y], x] + (c*x + d)*D[w[x, y], y] == alpha*w[x, y] + beta;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1199*)

pde =  (a*x + b)*D[w[x, y], x] + (c*y + d)*D[w[x, y], y] == alpha*w[x, y] + beta;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1200*)

pde =  (a*x + b)*D[w[x, y], x] + (c*y + d)*D[w[x, y], y] == alpha*w[x, y] + beta*y + gamma*x;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];


(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1201*)

pde =  (a*x + b)*D[w[x, y], x] + (c*x + d*y)*D[w[x, y], y] == alpha*w[x, y] + beta;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1202*)

pde = (a1*x + a0)*D[w[x, y], x] + (b2*y + b1*x + b0)*D[w[x, y], y] == (c2*y + c1*x + c0)*w[x, y] + k2*y + k1*x + k0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1203*)

pde = a*y*D[w[x, y], x] + (b1*x + b0)*D[w[x, y], y] == (c1*x + c0)*w[x, y] + s1*x + s0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1204*)

pde =  a*D[w[x, y], x] + b*D[w[x, y], y] == c*w[x, y] + beta*x*y + gamma;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1205*)

pde =  a*D[w[x, y], x] + b*D[w[x, y], y] == c*w[x, y] + x*(beta*x + gamma*y) + delta;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1206*)


 
 
 
pde =  x*D[w[x, y], x] + y*D[w[x, y], y] == w[x, y] + a*x^2 + b*y^2 + c;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1207*)


 
 
 
pde =  a*x*D[w[x, y], x] + b*y*D[w[x, y], y] == c*w[x, y] + x*(beta*x + gamma*y) + delta;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1208*)


 
 
 
pde =  a*y*D[w[x, y], x] + (b2*x^2 + b1*x + b0)*D[w[x, y], y] == (c2*x^2 + c1*x + c0)*w[x, y] + s2*x^2 + s1*x + s0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1209*)


 
 
 
pde =  a*y^2*D[w[x, y], x] + (b1*x^2 + b0)*D[w[x, y], y] == (c1*x^2 + c0)*w[x, y] + s1*x^2 + s0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1210*)

pde = (a1*x^2 + a0)*y^2*D[w[x, y], x] + (y + b2*x^2 + b1*x + b0)*D[w[x, y], y] == (c2*y + c1*x + c0)*w[x, y] + k22*y^2 + k12*x*y + k11*x^2 + k0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1211*)

pde = (a1*x^2 + a0)*y^2*D[w[x, y], x] + (b2*y^2 + b1*x^2)*D[w[x, y], y] == (c2*y^2 + c1*x^2)*w[x, y] + s22*y^2 + s12*x*y + s11*x^2 + s0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1212*)


 
 
 
pde =  a*x*D[w[x, y], x] + b*y*D[w[x, y], y] == alpha*y*w[x, y] + beta*Sqrt[x*y] + gamma;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];


(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1213*)


 
 
 
pde =  a*x*D[w[x, y], x] + b*y*D[w[x, y], y] == lambda*Sqrt[x*y]*w[x, y] + beta*x*y + gamma;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1214*)


 
 
 
pde =  a*y*D[w[x, y], x] + b*x*D[w[x, y], y] == alpha*w[x, y] + beta*Sqrt[x] + gamma;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1215*)


 
 
 
pde =  a*y*D[w[x, y], x] + b*x*D[w[x, y], y] == alpha*w[x, y] + beta*Sqrt[x] + gamma;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1216*)

pde =  a*Sqrt[x]*D[w[x, y], x] + b*Sqrt[y]*D[w[x, y], y] == alpha*w[x, y] + beta*x + gamma*y + delta;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1217*)

pde =  a*Sqrt[x]*D[w[x, y], x] + b*Sqrt[y]*D[w[x, y], y] == alpha*w[x, y] + beta*Sqrt[x] + gamma;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];


(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1218*)


 
 
 
pde =  a*Sqrt[y]*D[w[x, y], x] + b*Sqrt[x]*D[w[x, y], y] == alpha*w[x, y] + beta*Sqrt[x] + gamma;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1219*)


 
 
 
pde =  a*D[w[x, y], x] + b*D[w[x, y], y] == c*w[x, y] + k*x^n*y^m;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1220*)


 
 
 
pde =  a*D[w[x, y], x] + y*D[w[x, y], y] == b*w[x, y] + c*x^n*y^m;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];


(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1221*)


 
 
 
pde =  x*D[w[x, y], x] + y*D[w[x, y], y] == a*x*w[x, y] + b*x^n*y^m;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];


(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1222*)


 
 
 
pde =  x*D[w[x, y], x] + y*D[w[x, y], y] == a*Sqrt[x^2+y^2]*w[x,y] + b*x^n*y^m;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];


(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1223*)


 
 
 
pde =  a*x*D[w[x, y], x] + b*y*D[w[x, y], y] == c*x^n*y^m*w[x,y] + p*x^k*y^s;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];


(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1224*)


 
 
 
pde =  a*x*D[w[x, y], x] + b*y*D[w[x, y], y] == c*(x^n+p*y^m)*w[x,y] + q*x^k*y^s;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];


(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1225*)


 
 
 
pde =  x^2*D[w[x, y], x] + a*x*y*D[w[x, y], y] == b*y^2*w[x,y] + c*x^n*y^m;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];


(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1226*)

pde =  x^2*D[w[x, y], x] + x*y*D[w[x, y], y] == y^2*(a*x+b*y)*w[x,y] + c*x^n*y^m;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];


(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1227*)


 
 
 
pde =  a*x^n*D[w[x, y], x] + b*x^m*y*D[w[x, y], y] == c*x^p*y^q*w[x,y] + s*x^gamma*y^delta+d;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];


(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1228*)


 
 
 
pde =  a*x^n*D[w[x, y], x] + (b*x^m*y+x*x^k)*D[w[x, y], y] == s*x^p*y^q*w[x,y] + d;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];


(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1229*)

sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];


(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1230*)


 
 
 
pde =  a*y^k*D[w[x, y], x] + b*x^n*D[w[x, y], y] == c*w[x,y] + s*x^m;

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1231*)

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1287*)


pde =  a*y^k*D[w[x, y], x] + b*x^n*D[w[x, y], y] == c*w[x,y]+s*Log[beta*x]^m;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1288*)

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1358*)

sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y], {x, y}], 60*10]];

(*PDE number 1416*)

pde = (f[0]*x - f[1])*D[w[x, y,z], x] +(f[0]*y-f[2])*D[w[x, y,z], y] +(f[0]*z -f[3])*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;

sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];


(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1421*)


 
 
 
pde =  b*y*(3*a*x^2+ b*y^2+c*z^2)*D[w[x, y,z], x] - 2*a*x*(a*x^2+c*z^2)*D[w[x, y,z], y] + 2*a*b*x*y*z*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];


(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1422*)


 
 
 
pde =  b*(a*(a^2*x^2+b^2*y^2-1)*x+ b*y )*D[w[x, y,z], x] +a*(b*(a^2*x^2+b^2*y^2-1)*y - a*x)*D[w[x, y,z], y] + 2*a*b*z*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];


(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1423*)


 
 
 
pde =  x*(b^3*y^3 - 2*a^3*x^3)*D[w[x, y,z], x] +y*(2*b^3*y^3 -a^3*x^3)*D[w[x, y,z], y] +9*z*(a^3*x^3-b^3*y^3)*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];


(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1424*)


 
 
 
pde =  a*x^2*(a*b*x*y-c^2*z^2)*D[w[x, y,z], x] +a*x*y*(a*b*x*y-c^2*z^2)*D[w[x, y,z], y] +b*y*z*(b*c*y*z+2*a^2*x^2) *D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];

 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1425*)


 
 
 
pde =  x*(c*z^4 - b*y^4)*D[w[x, y,z], x] +y*(a*x^4-2*c*z^4)*D[w[x, y,z], y] +z*(2*b*y^4-a*x^4) *D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];


(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1426*)


 
 
 
pde =  x*D[w[x, y,z], x] +y*D[w[x, y,z], y] +a*Sqrt[x^2+y^2]*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];

 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1427*)


 
 
 
pde =  x*D[w[x, y,z], x] +y*D[w[x, y,z], y] +(z-a*Sqrt[x^2+y^2+z^2])*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];

 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1428*)


 
 
 
pde =  z*Sqrt[y^2+z^2]*D[w[x, y,z], x] +a*z*Sqrt[x^2+z^2]*D[w[x, y,z], y] -(x*Sqrt[y^2+z^2]+a*y*Sqrt[x^2+z^2])*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];


(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1429*)


 
 
 
 f[t_]:= a[6]*t^6+a[5]*t^5+a[4]*t^4+a[3]*t^3+a[2]*t^2+a[1]*t+a[0];
pde =  (y-z)*Sqrt[f[x]]*D[w[x, y,z], x] +(z-x)*Sqrt[f[y]]*D[w[x, y,z], y] +(x-y)*Sqrt[f[z]]*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];


(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1430*)


 
 
 
pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] +a*x^n*y^m*D[w[x, y,z], y] +b*x^nu *y^mu* z^lambda *D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];


(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1431*)

pde = D[w[x, y,z], x] +(a1*x^n1*y+b1*x^m1)*D[w[x, y,z], y] +(a2*x^n2*y+b2*x^m1)*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];

 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1432*)


 
 
 
pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] +(a1*x^n1*y+b1*x^m1)*D[w[x, y,z], y] +(a2*x^n2*z+b2*x^m1)*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];

 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1433*)


 
 
 
pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] +(a1*x^n1*y+b1*x^m1)*D[w[x, y,z], y] +(a2*x^n2*z+b2*y^m1)*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];

 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1434*)

pde = D[w[x, y,z], x] +(a1*x^n1*y+b1*x^m1*y^k1)*D[w[x, y,z], y] +(a2*x^n2*z+b2*x^m2*z^k1)*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];

 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1435*)

pde = a*x^n*D[w[x, y,z], x] +b*y^m*D[w[x, y,z], y] +c*z^L*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];

 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1436*)


 
 
 
pde =  a*y^m*D[w[x, y,z], x] +b*x^n*D[w[x, y,z], y] +c*z^L*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];


(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1437*)


 
 
 
pde =  x*(y^n-z^n)*D[w[x, y,z], x] +y*(z^n-x^n)*D[w[x, y,z], y] +z*(x^n-y^n)*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];


(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1438*)


 
 
 
pde =  a*D[w[x, y,z], x] +b*Exp[alpha*x]*D[w[x, y,z], y] +c*Exp[beta*y]*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];

 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1439*)


 
 
 
pde =  a*D[w[x, y,z], x] +b*Exp[alpha*x]*D[w[x, y,z], y] +c*Exp[gamma*z]*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];

 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1440*)


 
 
 
pde =  a*D[w[x, y,z], x] +b*Exp[beta*y]*D[w[x, y,z], y] +c*Exp[gamma*z]*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];

 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1441*)

pde = D[w[x, y,z], x] +(A1*Exp[alpha1*x]+B1*Exp[nu1*x+lambda*y])*D[w[x, y,z], y] +(A2*Exp[alpha2*x]+B2*Exp[nu2*x+beta*y])*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];


(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1442*)


 
 
 
pde =  a*Exp[alpha*x]*D[w[x, y,z], x] +b*Exp[beta*y]*D[w[x, y,z], y] +c*Exp[gamma*z]*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];

 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1443*)


 
 
 
pde =  a*Exp[beta*y]*D[w[x, y,z], x] +b*Exp[alpha*x]*D[w[x, y,z], y] +c*Exp[gamma*z]*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];

 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1444*)

pde = (a1+a2*Exp[alpha*x])*D[w[x, y,z], x] +(b1+b2*Exp[beta*y])*D[w[x, y,z], y] +(c1+c2*Exp[gamma*z])*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];

 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1445*)


 
 
 
pde =  Exp[beta*y]*(a1+a2*Exp[alpha*x])*D[w[x, y,z], x] +Exp[alpha*x]*(b1+b2*Exp[beta*y])*D[w[x, y,z], y] +c*Exp[beta*y+gamma*z]*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];

 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1446*)


 
 
 
pde =  a*y*Exp[alpha*x]*D[w[x, y,z], x] + b*Exp[beta*y]*D[w[x, y,z], y] +c*Exp[gamma*z]*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];


(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1447*)


 
 
 
pde =  a*y*Exp[alpha*x]*D[w[x, y,z], x] + b*Exp[beta*y]*D[w[x, y,z], y] +c*z*Exp[gamma*z]*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];


(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1448*)


 
 
 
pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + ( y^2+ a*Exp[alpha*x]*(alpha-a*Exp[alpha*x]))*D[w[x, y,z], y] +(z^2 +b*z +c*Exp[beta*x]*(beta - b -c*Exp[beta*x]))*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];

 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1449*)


 
 
 
pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + ( y^2+ b*y + a*Exp[alpha*x]*(alpha-b-a*Exp[alpha*x]))*D[w[x, y,z], y] +(z^2 +c*Exp[beta*x]*(z-k)-k^2)*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];

 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1450*)


 
 
 
pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + ( y^2+ b*y + a*Exp[alpha*x]*(y-b)-b^2)*D[w[x, y,z], y] +(z^2 +c*(x*z-1)*Exp[beta*x])*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];


(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1451*)


 
 
 
pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + ( y^2- a*Exp[alpha*x]*(x*y-1))*D[w[x, y,z], y] +(c*Exp[beta*x]*z^2+b*Exp[-beta*x])*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];


(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1452*)


 
 
 
pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + ( a*y^2- a*Exp[alpha*x]+ b * Exp[-alpha*x])*D[w[x, y,z], y] +(d*Exp[beta*x]*z^2+c*Exp[gamma*x]*(gamma- c*d*Exp[(beta+gamma)*x]))*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];


(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1453*)


 
 
 
pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + (b*Exp[alpha*x]*y^2 + a*Exp[beta*x]*(beta- a*b*Exp[(alpha+beta)*x]))*D[w[x, y,z], y] +(c*z^2*Exp[gamma*x]+ d*z + k*Exp[-gamma*x])*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];


(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1454*)


 
 
 
pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + (a*Exp[alpha*x]*y^2 + b*y +  c*Exp[alpha*x])*D[w[x, y,z], y] +(Exp[beta*x]*z^2+ d*Exp[gamma*x]*(z+beta*Exp[-beta*x]))*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];


(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1455*)


 
 
 
pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + ( Exp[alpha*x]*y^2 + a*y*Exp[beta*x] + a*alpha*Exp[(beta-alpha)*x])*D[w[x, y,z], y] +(gamma*Exp[gamma*x]*z^2+ b*Exp[delta*x]*(z+Exp[-gamma*x]))*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];


(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1456*)


 
 
 
pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + ( alpha*Exp[alpha*x]*y^2 + a*Exp[beta*x]*(y+Exp[-alpha*x]))*D[w[x, y,z], y] +(Exp[gamma*x]*(z-b*Exp[delta*x])^2+b*delta*Exp[delta*x])*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];


(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1457*)


 
 
 
pde =  x*D[w[x, y,z], x] + ( a1*Exp[alpha*x]*y^2 + beta*y+ a1*b2^2*x^(2*beta)*Exp[alpha*x])*D[w[x, y,z], y] +(a2*x^(2*n)*z^2*Exp[lamba*x]+(b2*x^n*Exp[lambda*x] - n)*z + c*Exp[lambda*x])*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];


(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1458*)

pde = D[w[x, y,z], x] +( a1*Exp[lambda1*x]*y + b1*Exp[beta1*x]*y^k)*D[w[x, y,z], y] +(a2*Exp[lamba2*x]*z + b2*Exp[beta1*x]*z^m) *D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];

 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1459*)

pde = D[w[x, y,z], x] +( a1*Exp[beta1*x]*y + b1*Exp[gamma1*x]*y^k)*D[w[x, y,z], y] +(a2*Exp[beta2*x] + b2*Exp[gamma1*x+lambda*z]) *D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];


(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1460*)

pde = D[w[x, y,z], x] +( a1*x^n+b1*x^m*Exp[lambda*y])*D[w[x, y,z], y] +(a2*x^k+b2*x^2*Exp[beta*z]) *D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];

 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1461*)


 
 
 
pde =  (a*x^n*Exp[lambda*y] + b*x*y^m)*D[w[x, y,z], x] +Exp[mu*y]*D[w[x, y,z], y] +(c*y^L*z^k+d*y^p*z) *D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];


(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1462*)


 
 
 
pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + (y^2 + 2*a*alpha*Exp[alpha*x^2]-a^2*Exp[2*alpha*x^2] )*D[w[x, y,z], y] +( c*Exp[-2*beta*x^2]*z^2 + 2*beta*x*z + b^2*c)*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];


(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1463*)


 
 
 
pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + (a*Exp[2*alpha*x^2]*y^2 + 2*alpha*x*y + a*b^2 )*D[w[x, y,z], y] +( c*x^beta*z^2 + 2*gamma*x*z + c*d^2*x^beta*Exp[2*gamma*x^2])*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];


(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1464*)


 
 
 
pde =  a*D[w[x, y,z], x] + b*D[w[x, y,z], y] +c*Sinh[lambda*x]*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];

 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1465*)


 
 
 
pde =  a*D[w[x, y,z], x] + b*Sinh[beta*y]*D[w[x, y,z], y] +c*Sinh[lambda*x]*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];

 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1466*)


 
 
 
pde =  a*D[w[x, y,z], x] + b*Sinh[beta*y]*D[w[x, y,z], y] +c*Sinh[gamma*z]*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];

 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1467*)


 
 
 
pde =  a*Sinh[lambda*x]*D[w[x, y,z], x] + b*Sinh[beta*y]*D[w[x, y,z], y] +c*Sinh[gamma*z]*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];

 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1468*)


 
 
 
pde =  a*Sinh[beta*y]*D[w[x, y,z], x] + b*Sinh[lambda*x]*D[w[x, y,z], y] +c*Sinh[gamma*z]*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];


(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1469*)


 
 
 
pde =  a*Sinh[beta*y]*D[w[x, y,z], x] + b*Sinh[lambda*x]*D[w[x, y,z], y] +c*Sinh[lambda*x]*Sinh[beta*y]*Sinh[gamma*z]*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];


(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1470*)


 
 
 
pde =  a*D[w[x, y,z], x] + b*D[w[x, y,z], y] +c*Cosh[beta*x]*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];

 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1471*)


 
 
 
pde =  a*D[w[x, y,z], x] + b*Cosh[beta*x]*D[w[x, y,z], y] +c*Cosh[lambda*x]*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];

 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1472*)


 
 
 
pde =  a*D[w[x, y,z], x] + b*Cosh[beta*y]*D[w[x, y,z], y] +c*Cosh[lambda*x]*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];

 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1473*)


 
 
 
pde =  a*D[w[x, y,z], x] + b*Cosh[beta*y]*D[w[x, y,z], y] +c*Cosh[gamma*z]*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];

 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1474*)


 
 
 
pde =  a*Cosh[lambda*x]*D[w[x, y,z], x] + b*Cosh[beta*y]*D[w[x, y,z], y] +c*Cosh[gamma*z]*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];

 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1475*)


 
 
 
pde =  a*Cosh[beta*y]*D[w[x, y,z], x] + b*Cosh[lambda*x]*D[w[x, y,z], y] +c*Cosh[gamma*z]*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];


(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1476*)


 
 
 
pde =  a*D[w[x, y,z], x] + b*D[w[x, y,z], y] +c*Tanh[gamma*z]*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];

 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1477*)


 
 
 
pde =  a*D[w[x, y,z], x] + b*Tanh[beta*x]*D[w[x, y,z], y] +c*Tanh[lambda*x]*D[w[x,y,z],z]==0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1605*)


 
 
 
pde =  a*x*D[w[x, y,z], x] + b*y*D[w[x, y,z], y] +c*z*D[w[x,y,z],z]== alpha*x^n+beta*y^m+gamma*z^k;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1606*)


 
 
 
pde =  x*D[w[x, y,z], x] + a*z*D[w[x, y,z], y] +b*y*D[w[x,y,z],z]== c*x^n;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1607*)


 
 
 
pde =  a*b*x*D[w[x, y,z], x] + b*(a*y+b*z)*D[w[x, y,z], y] +a*(a*y-b*z)*D[w[x,y,z],z]== c*x^n;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1608*)


 
 
 
pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + a*x^n*y^m*D[w[x, y,z], y] +b*x^nu*y^mu*z^lambda*D[w[x,y,z],z]== c*x^k;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1609*)

pde = D[w[x, y,z], x] + (a1*x^n1*y+ b1*x^m1)*D[w[x, y,z], y] +(a2*x^n2*y+b2*x^m2)*D[w[x,y,z],z]== c2*x^k2*y+c1*x^k1*z;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1610*)


 
 
 
 
pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + (a1*x^n1*y+ b1*x^m1)*D[w[x, y,z], y] +(a2*x^n2*z+b2*x^m2)*D[w[x,y,z],z]== c2*x^k2*y+c1*x^k1*z;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1611*)


 
 
 
 
pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + (a1*x^n1*y+ b1*y^k)*D[w[x, y,z], y] +(a2*x^n2*z+b2*z^m)*D[w[x,y,z],z]== c*x^s;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1612*)

pde = D[w[x, y,z], x] + (a1*x^n1*y+ b1*y^k)*D[w[x, y,z], y] +(a2*x^n2*z+b2*z^m)*D[w[x,y,z],z]== c1*x^s1+c2*y^s2;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1613*)


 
 
 
 
pde =  x*D[w[x, y,z], x] + y*D[w[x, y,z], y] +a*Sqrt[x^2+y^2]*D[w[x,y,z],z]== b*x^n;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1614*)


 
 
 
 
pde =  x*D[w[x, y,z], x] + y*D[w[x, y,z], y] +(z-a*Sqrt[x^2+y^2+z^2])*D[w[x,y,z],z]== b*x^n;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1615*)


 
 
 
 
pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + a*Exp[lambda*x]*D[w[x, y,z], y] +b*Exp[beta*x]*D[w[x,y,z],z]== c*Exp[gamma*x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1616*)


 
 
 
 
pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + a*Exp[lambda*x]*D[w[x, y,z], y] +b*Exp[beta*y]*D[w[x,y,z],z]== c*Exp[gamma*y]+s*Exp[mu*z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1617*)


 
 
 
 
pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + a*Exp[lambda*y]*D[w[x, y,z], y] +b*Exp[beta*y]*D[w[x,y,z],z]== c*Exp[gamma*x]+s*Exp[mu*z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1618*)


 
 
 
 
pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + (A1*Exp[alpha1*x] +B1*Exp[nu1*x+lambda*y] )*D[w[x, y,z], y] +(A2*Exp[alpha2*x] +B2*Exp[nu2*x+beta*y] )*D[w[x,y,z],z]== k*Exp[gamma*z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1619*)


 
 
 
 
pde =  a*Exp[alpha*x]*D[w[x, y,z], x] + b*Exp[beta*y]*D[w[x, y,z], y] +c*Exp[gamma*z]*D[w[x,y,z],z]== k*Exp[lambda*x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1620*)


 
 
 
 
pde =  a*Exp[beta*x]*D[w[x, y,z], x] + b*Exp[alpha*x]*D[w[x, y,z], y] +c*Exp[gamma*z]*D[w[x,y,z],z]== k*Exp[lambda*x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1621*)


 
 
 
 
pde =  (a1+a2*Exp[alpha*x])*D[w[x, y,z], x] + (b1+b2*Exp[beta*y])*D[w[x, y,z], y] +(c1+c2*Exp[gamma*z])*D[w[x,y,z],z]== k1+k2*Exp[alpha*x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1622*)

pde = Exp[beta*y]*(a1+a2*Exp[alpha*x])*D[w[x, y,z], x] + Exp[alpha*x]*(b1+b2*Exp[beta*y])*D[w[x, y,z], y] +c*Exp[beta*y+gamma*z]*D[w[x,y,z],z]== k3*Exp[beta*y]*(k1+k2*Exp[alpha*x]);
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1623*)


 
 
 
 
pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + a*x^n*D[w[x, y,z], y] +b*x^m*D[w[x,y,z],z]== c*Exp[lambda*x]*y+k*Exp[beta*x]*z+s*Exp[gamma*x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1624*)


 
 
 
 
pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + a*Exp[lambda*x]*D[w[x, y,z], y] +b*x^m*D[w[x,y,z],z]== c*x^n*y+k*Exp[beta*x]*z+s*Exp[gamma*x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1625*)


 
 
 
 
pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + a*Exp[lambda*x]*D[w[x, y,z], y] +b*y*D[w[x,y,z],z]== k*Exp[beta*x]*z+s*Exp[gamma*x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1626*)


 
 
 
 
pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + a*y^n*D[w[x, y,z], y] +b*z^m*D[w[x,y,z],z]== c*Exp[lambda*x]+k*Exp[beta*y]+s*Exp[gamma*z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1627*)


 
 
 
 
pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + a*Exp[beta*y]*D[w[x, y,z], y] +b*z^m*D[w[x,y,z],z]== x*Exp[lambda*x]+k*y^n+s*Exp[gamma*z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1628*)


 
 
 
 
pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + ( y^2+b*y+a*Exp[alpha*x]*(y-b)-b^2 )*D[w[x, y,z], y] +( z^2+c*(x*z-1)* Exp[beta*x])*D[w[x,y,z],z]== k*Exp[gamma*x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1629*)


 
 
 
 
pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + ( y^2+a*Exp[alpha*x]*(x+1))*D[w[x, y,z], y] +( c*Exp[beta*x]*z^2+b*Exp[-beta*x])*D[w[x,y,z],z]== k*Exp[lambda*x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1630*)


 
 
 
 
pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + ( a*Exp[alpha*x]*y^2+b*Exp[-alpha*x])*D[w[x, y,z], y] + ( d*Exp[beta*x]*z^2 +c*Exp[gamma*x]*(gamma-c*d*Exp[(beta+gamma)*x]))*D[w[x,y,z],z]== k*Exp[lambda*x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];

(*PDE number 1631*)


 
 
 
 
pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + ( a1*Exp[lambda1*x]*y+ b1*Exp[beta1*x]*y^k)*D[w[x, y,z], y] +  ( a2*Exp[lambda2*x]*z +b2*Exp[beta2*x]*z^m)*D[w[x,y,z],z]== c*x^s;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1632*)


 
 
 
 
pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + ( a1*Exp[beta1*x]*y+ b1*Exp[gamma1*x]*y^k)*D[w[x, y,z], y] +  ( a2*Exp[beta2*x]*z +b2*Exp[gamma1*x+lambda*z])*D[w[x,y,z],z]== c*x^s;

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1633*)

pde = D[w[x, y,z], x] + (a1*x^n+b1*x^m*Exp[lambda*y] )*D[w[x, y,z], y] +  ( a2*x^k+b2*x^L*Exp[beta*z])*D[w[x,y,z],z]== c*x^s;

(*PDE number 1634*)

sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1722*)


 
 
 
 
 
pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + f[x]*D[w[x, y,z], y] + g[x]*D[w[x,y,z],z]== h2[x]*y+h1[x]*z+h0[x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1723*)


 
 
 
 
 
pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + f[x]*(y+a)*D[w[x, y,z], y] + g[x]*(z+b)*D[w[x,y,z],z]== h[x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1724*)


 
 
 
 
 
pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + (a*y+f[x])*D[w[x, y,z], y] + (b*z+g[x])*D[w[x,y,z],z]== h[x];
