(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1725*)


 
 
 
 
 
pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + (f1[x]*y+f2[x])*D[w[x, y,z], y] + (g1[x]*y+g2[x])*D[w[x,y,z],z]== h2[x]*y+h1[x]*z+h0[x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];

(*PDE number 1726*)


 
 
 
 
 
pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + (f1[x]*z+f2[x])*D[w[x, y,z], y] + (g1[x]*y+g2[x])*D[w[x,y,z],z]== h2[x]*y+h1[x]*z+h0[x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x, y,z}], 60*10]];

(*PDE number 1727*)


 
 
 
 
 
pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + (y^2-a^2 + a *lambda*Sin[lambda*x]-a^2*Sinh[lambda*x]^2)*D[w[x, y,z], y] + f[x]*Sinh[gamma*z]*D[w[x,y,z],z]== g[x];

(*PDE number 1780*)


pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + (a1*x^n1*y+b1*x^m1)*D[w[x, y,z], y] +(a2*x^n2*y+b2*x^m2)*D[w[x,y,z],z]== (c1*x^k2*y+c1*x^k1*z)*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1781*)


pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + (a1*x^n1*y+b1*x^m1)*D[w[x, y,z], y] +(a2*x^n2*z+b2*x^m2)*D[w[x,y,z],z]== (c1*x^k2*y+c1*x^k1*z)*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1782*)


pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + (a1*x^n1*y+b1*y^k)*D[w[x, y,z], y] +(a2*x^n2*z+b2*z^m)*D[w[x,y,z],z]== c*x^s*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1783*)


pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + (a1*x^n1*y+b1*y^k)*D[w[x, y,z], y] +(a2*y^n2*z+b2*z^m)*D[w[x,y,z],z]== (c1*x^s1+c2*y^s2+c3*z^s3)*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1784*)


pde =  x*D[w[x, y,z], x] + y*D[w[x, y,z], y] +a*Sqrt[x^2+y^2]*D[w[x,y,z],z]== b*x^n*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1785*)


pde =  x*D[w[x, y,z], x] + y*D[w[x, y,z], y] +(z-a*Sqrt[x^2+y^2+z^2])*D[w[x,y,z],z]== b*x^n*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1786*)


pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + a*Exp[lambda*x]*D[w[x, y,z], y] +b*Exp[beta*x]*D[w[x,y,z],z]== c*Exp[gamma*x]*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1787*)


pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + a*Exp[lambda*x]*D[w[x, y,z], y] +b*Exp[beta*x]*D[w[x,y,z],z]== (c*Exp[gamma*y]+s Exp[mu*z])*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1788*)


pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + a*Exp[lambda*y]*D[w[x, y,z], y] +b*Exp[beta*y]*D[w[x,y,z],z]== (c*Exp[gamma*x]+s Exp[mu*z])*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1789*)


pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + (A1*Exp[alpha1*x] + B1*Exp[nu1*x+lambda*y])*D[w[x, y,z], y] + (A2*Exp[alpha2*x] + B2*Exp[nu2*x+beta*z])*D[w[x,y,z],z]== k*Exp[gamma*x]*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1790*)


pde =  a*Exp[alpha*x]*D[w[x, y,z], x] + b*Exp[beta*y]*D[w[x, y,z], y] + c*Exp[gamma*z]*D[w[x,y,z],z]== k*Exp[lambda*x]*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1791*)


pde =  a*Exp[beta*y]*D[w[x, y,z], x] + b*Exp[alpha*x]*D[w[x, y,z], y] + c*Exp[gamma*z]*D[w[x,y,z],z]== k*Exp[lambda*x]*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1792*)


pde =  (a1+a2*Exp[alpha*x])*D[w[x, y,z], x] + (b1+b2*Exp[beta*y])*D[w[x, y,z], y] + (c1+c2*Exp[gamma*z])*D[w[x,y,z],z]== (k1+k2*Exp[alpha*x])*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1793*)


pde =  Exp[beta*y]*(a1+a2*Exp[alpha*x])*D[w[x, y,z], x] + Exp[alpha*x]*(b1+b2*Exp[beta*y])*D[w[x, y,z], y] + c*Exp[beta*y+gamma*z]*D[w[x,y,z],z]== k3*Exp[beta*y]*(k1+k2*Exp[alpha*x])*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1794*)


pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + a*x^n*D[w[x, y,z], y] + b*x^m*D[w[x,y,z],z]== (c*Exp[lambda*x]*y+k*Exp[beta*x]*z+s*Exp[gamma*x])*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1795*)


pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + a*Exp[lambda*x]*D[w[x, y,z], y] + b*x^m*D[w[x,y,z],z]== (c*x^n*y+k*Exp[beta*x]*z+s*Exp[gamma*x])*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1796*)


pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + a*Exp[lambda*x]*D[w[x, y,z], y] + b*y*D[w[x,y,z],z]== (k*Exp[beta*x]*z+s*Exp[gamma*x])*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1797*)


pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + a*y^n*D[w[x, y,z], y] + b*z^m*D[w[x,y,z],z]== (c*Exp[lambda*x]+k*Exp[beta*y]+s*Exp[gamma*z])*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1798*)


pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + a*Exp[beta*y]*D[w[x, y,z], y] + b*z^m*D[w[x,y,z],z]== (c*Exp[lambda*x]+k*y^n+s*Exp[gamma*z])*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1799*)


pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + (y^2+ b*y+ a*Exp[alpha*y]*(y-b)-b^2)*D[w[x, y,z], y] +  (z^2+c*(x*z-1)*Exp[beta*x])*D[w[x,y,z],z]== k*Exp[lambda*x]*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1800*)


pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + (y^2+ a*Exp[alpha*x]*(x+1))*D[w[x, y,z], y] + (c*Exp[beta*x]*z^2+b*Exp[-beta*x])*D[w[x,y,z],z]== k*Exp[lambda*x]*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1801*)


pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + (a*Exp[alpha*x]*y^2+b*Exp[-alpha*x])*D[w[x, y,z], y] + (d*Exp[beta*x]*z^2 + c*Exp[gamma*x]*(gamma-c*d*Exp[(beta+gamma)*x]))*D[w[x,y,z],z]== k*Exp[lambda*x]*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1802*)


pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + (a1*Exp[lambda1*x]*y+b1*Exp[beta1*x]*y^k)*D[w[x, y,z], y] +  (a2*Exp[lambda2*x]*z+b2*Exp[beta2*x]*z^m)*D[w[x,y,z],z]== c*x^s*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1803*)


pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + (a1*Exp[beta1*x]*y+b1*Exp[gamma1*x]*y^k)*D[w[x, y,z], y] +  (a2*Exp[beta2*x]+b2*Exp[gamma2*x+lambda2*z])*D[w[x,y,z],z]== c*x^s*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1804*)


pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + (a1*x^n+b1*x^m*Exp[lambda*y] )*D[w[x, y,z], y] +   (a2*x^k+b2*x^r*Exp[beta*z])*D[w[x,y,z],z]== c*x^s*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1805*)


pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + a*D[w[x, y,z], y] +  b*D[w[x,y,z],z]== c*Sinh[beta*x]^n*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1806*)


pde =  a*D[w[x, y,z], x] + b*D[w[x, y,z], y] +  c*Sinh[lambda*x]*D[w[x,y,z],z]== (k*Sinh[beta*x]+s*Sinh[gamma*z])*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1807*)


pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + a*Sinh[beta*x]^n*D[w[x, y,z], y] +  b*Sinh[lambda*x]^k*D[w[x,y,z],z]== c*Sinh[gamma*x]^m *w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];

(*PDE number 1808*)


pde =  a*D[w[x, y,z], x] + b*Sinh[beta*y]*D[w[x, y,z], y] +  c*Sinh[lambda*x]*D[w[x,y,z],z]== k*Sinh[gamma*z] *w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1809*)


pde =  a1*Sinh[lambda1*x]^n1*D[w[x, y,z], x] + b1*Sinh[beta1*y]^m1*D[w[x, y,z], y] +  c1*Sinh[gamma1*x]^k1*D[w[x, y,z], z]== (a2*Sinh[lambda2*x]^n2+b2*Sinh[beta2*y]^m2+c2*Sinh[gamma2*x]^k2) *w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1810*)


pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + a*D[w[x, y,z], y] +  b*D[w[x,y,z],z]== c*Cosh[beta*x]^n*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];

sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];

pde =  a1*Cosh[lambda1*x]^n1*D[w[x, y,z], x] + b1*Cosh[beta1*y]^m1*D[w[x, y,z], y] +  c1*Cosh[gamma1*x]^k1*D[w[x, y,z], z]== (a2*Cosh[lambda2*x]^n2+b2*Cosh[beta2*y]^m2+c2*Cosh[gamma2*x]^k2) *w[x,y,z];

pde =  a1*Tanh[lambda1*x]^n1*D[w[x, y,z], x] + b1*Tanh[beta1*y]^m1*D[w[x, y,z], y] +  c1*Tanh[gamma1*x]^k1*D[w[x, y,z], z]== (a2*Tanh[lambda2*x]^n2+b2*Tanh[beta2*y]^m2+c2*Tanh[gamma2*x]^k2) *w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];

(*PDE number 1821*)


pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + a*D[w[x, y,z], y] +  b*D[w[x,y,z],z]== c*Coth[beta*x]^n*w[x,y,z];

sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1835*)


pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + a*Log[beta*x]^n*D[w[x, y,z], y] +  b*Log[lambda*y]^k*D[w[x,y,z],z]== c*Log[gamma*x]^m*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1836*)


pde =   a1*Log[lambda1*x]^n1*D[w[x, y,z], x] + b1*Log[beta1*y]^m1*D[w[x, y,z], x]*D[w[x, y,z], y] + c1*Log[gamma1*z]^k1*D[w[x,y,z],z]== ( a2*Log[lambda2*x]^n2+ b2*Log[beta2*y]^m2+ c2*Log[gamma2*z]^k2)*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1837*)


pde =  a*D[w[x, y,z], x] + b*D[w[x, y,z], y] +  c*x^n*Log[lambda*y]^k*D[w[x,y,z],z]== s*y^m*Log[beta*x]^r*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1838*)


pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + a*x^n*D[w[x, y,z], y] +  b*x^m*D[w[x,y,z],z]== (c*y*Log[lambda*x]^k+s*z*Log[beta*x]^r)*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1839*)


pde =  D[w[x, y,z], x] + a*Log[lambda*x]^n*D[w[x, y,z], y] +  b*y^m*D[w[x,y,z],z]== (c*Log[beta*x]^k+s*Log[gamma*z]^r)*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1840*)


pde =  a*Log[lambda*x]^n*D[w[x, y,z], x] + z*D[w[x, y,z], y] +  b*Log[lambda*y]^k*D[w[x,y,z],z]== (c*x^m+s*Log[gamma*y])*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1841*)


pde =  a*x*Log[x]^n*D[w[x, y,z], x] + b*y*Log[y]^m*D[w[x, y,z], y] +  c*z*Log[z]^r*D[w[x,y,z],z]== k*Log[x]^s*w[x,y,z];

sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1891*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+f[x]*D[w[x,y,z],y]+g[x]*D[w[x,y,z],z]==(h2[x]*y+h1[x]*z+h0[x])*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1892*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+f[x]*(y+a)*D[w[x,y,z],y]+g[x]*(z+b)*D[w[x,y,z],z]==h[x]*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1893*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+(a*y+f[x])*D[w[x,y,z],y]+(b*z+g[x])*D[w[x,y,z],z]==h[x]*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1894*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+(f1[x]*y+f2[x])*D[w[x,y,z],y]+(g1[x]*y+g2[x])*D[w[x,y,z],z]==(h2[x]*y+h1[x]*z+h0[x])*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1895*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+(f1[x]*y+f2[x])*D[w[x,y,z],y]+(g1[x]*z+g2[x])*D[w[x,y,z],z]==(h2[x]*y+h1[x]*z+h0[x])*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1896*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+(y^2-a^2+a*lambda*Sinh[lambda*x]-a^2*Sinh[lambda*x]^2)*D[w[x,y,z],y]+f[x]*Sinh[gamma*z]*D[w[x,y,z],z]==g[x]*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1897*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+( f1[x]*y+f2[x]*y^k)*D[w[x,y,z],y]+(g1[x]*z+g2[x]*z^m)*D[w[x,y,z],z]==h[x]*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1898*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+( f1[x]*y+f2[x]*y^k)*D[w[x,y,z],y]+(g1[x]+g2[x]*Exp[lambda*z])*D[w[x,y,z],z]==h[x]*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1899*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+( f1[x]+f2[x]*Exp[lambda*y])*D[w[x,y,z],y]+(g1[x]+g2[x]*Exp[beta*z])*D[w[x,y,z],z]==h[x]*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1900*)


pde =  f[x]*D[w[x,y,z],x]+g[y]*D[w[x,y,z],y]+h[z]*D[w[x,y,z],z]==(varphi[z]+psi[y]+chi[z])*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1901*)


pde =  f[x]*D[w[x,y,z],x]+z*D[w[x,y,z],y]+g[y]*D[w[x,y,z],z]==(h2[x]+h1[y])*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1902*)


pde =  f1[x]*D[w[x,y,z],x]+f2[x]*g[y]*D[w[x,y,z],y]+f3[x]*h[z]*D[w[x,y,z],z]==f4[x]*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1903*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+(f1[x]*y+f2[x])*D[w[x,y,z],y]+(g1[x]*z+g2[x])*D[w[x,y,z],z]==(h1[x]+h2[y])*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1904*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+(f1[x]*y+f2[x]*y^k)*D[w[x,y,z],y]+(g1[y]*z+g2[x])*D[w[x,y,z],z]==(h1[x]+h2[y])*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1905*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+(f1[x]*y+f2[x]*y^k)*D[w[x,y,z],y]+(g1[x]+g2[x]*Exp[lambda*z])*D[w[x,y,z],z]==(h1[x]+h2[y])*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1906*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+(f1[x]*y+f2[x]*Exp[lambda*y])*D[w[x,y,z],y]+(g1[y]*z+g2[x]*z^k)*D[w[x,y,z],z]==(h1[x]+h2[y])*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1907*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+(f1[x]*y+f2[x]*Exp[lambda*y])*D[w[x,y,z],y]+(g1[x]+g2[y]*Exp[beta*z])*D[w[x,y,z],z]==(h1[x]+h2[y])*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1908*)


pde =  a*D[w[x,y,z],x]+b*D[w[x,y,z],y]+f[x,y]*D[w[x,y,z],z]==g[x,y]*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1909*)


pde =  a*D[w[x,y,z],x]+b*D[w[x,y,z],y]+f[x,y]*g[z]*D[w[x,y,z],z]==h[x,y]*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1910*)


pde =  x*D[w[x,y,z],x]+y*D[w[x,y,z],y]+(z+f[x,y])*D[w[x,y,z],z]==g[x,y]*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1911*)


pde =  a*x*D[w[x,y,z],x]+b*y*D[w[x,y,z],y]+f[x,y]*g[z]*D[w[x,y,z],z]==h[x,y]*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1912*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+(f1[x]*y+f2[x])*D[w[x,y,z],y]+(g1[x,y]*z+g2[x,y])*D[w[x,y,z],z]==h[x,y,z]*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1913*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+(f1[x]*y+f2[x]*y^k)*D[w[x,y,z],y]+(g1[x,y]*z+g2[x,y]*z^m)*D[w[x,y,z],z]==h[x,y,z]*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1914*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+(f1[x]*y+f2[x]*y^k)*D[w[x,y,z],y]+(g1[x,y]*z+g2[x,y]*Exp[lambda*z])*D[w[x,y,z],z]==h[x,y,z]*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1915*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+(f1[x]*y+f2[x]*Exp[lambda*y])*D[w[x,y,z],y]+(g1[x,y]*z+g2[x,y]*z^k)*D[w[x,y,z],z]==h[x,y,z]*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1916*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+(f1[x]*y+f2[x]*Exp[lambda*y])*D[w[x,y,z],y]+(g1[x,y]*z+g2[x,y]*Exp[beta*z])*D[w[x,y,z],z]==h[x,y,z]*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1917*)


pde =  f1[x]*g1[y]*D[w[x,y,z],x]+f2[x]*g2[y]*D[w[x,y,z],y]+(h1[x,y]+h2[x,y]*z^m)*D[w[x,y,z],z]==h3[x,y,z]*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1918*)


pde =  f1[x]*g1[y]*D[w[x,y,z],x]+f2[x]*g2[y]*D[w[x,y,z],y]+(h1[x,y]+h2[x,y]*Exp[lambda*z])*D[w[x,y,z],z]==h3[x,y,z]*w[x,y,z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1919*)


pde =  a*D[w[x,y,z],x]+b*D[w[x,y,z],y]+c*D[w[x,y,z],z]==(alpha*x+beta)*w[x,y,z]+p*x+q;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1920*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+a*z*D[w[x,y,z],y]+b*y*D[w[x,y,z],z]==(c*x+k)*w[x,y,z]+p*x+q;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1921*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+(a1*x+a0)*D[w[x,y,z],y]+(b1*x+b0)*D[w[x,y,z],z]==(c1*x+c0)*w[x,y,z]+s1*x+s0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1922*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+(b1*x+b0)*D[w[x,y,z],y]+(c1*y+c0)*D[w[x,y,z],z]==a*w[x,y,z]+s1*x+s0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1923*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+(a*y+k1*x+k0)*D[w[x,y,z],y]+(b*z+n1*x+n0)*D[w[x,y,z],z]==(c1*x+c0)*w[x,y,z]+s1*x+s0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1924*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+(a2*y+a1*x+a0)*D[w[x,y,z],y]+(b3*z+b2*y+b1*x+b0)*D[w[x,y,z],z]==(c3*z+c2*y+c1*x+c0)*w[x,y,z]+s3*z+s2*y+s1*x+s0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1925*)


pde =  a*x*D[w[x,y,z],x]+b*x*D[w[x,y,z],y]+c*z*D[w[x,y,z],z]==(alpha*x+beta)*w[x,y,z]+p*x+q;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1926*)


pde =  a*x*D[w[x,y,z],x]+b*y*D[w[x,y,z],y]+c*z*D[w[x,y,z],z]==(alpha*x+beta)*w[x,y,z]+p*x+q;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1927*)


pde =  x*D[w[x,y,z],x]+a*z*D[w[x,y,z],y]+b*y*D[w[x,y,z],z]==(c*x+k)*w[x,y,z]+p*x+q;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1928*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+(a1*x^2+a0)*D[w[x,y,z],y]+(b1*x^2+b0)*D[w[x,y,z],z]==(c1*x+c0)*w[x,y,z]+s1*x^2+s0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1929*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+(b1*x^2+b0)*D[w[x,y,z],y]+(c1*y^2+c0)*D[w[x,y,z],z]==a*w[x,y,z]+s1*x^2+s0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1930*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+(a*y+k1*x^2+k0)*D[w[x,y,z],y]+(b*z+n1*x^2+n0)*D[w[x,y,z],z]==(c1*x+c0)*w[x,y,z]+s1*x+s0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1931*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+(a2*x*y+a1*x+a0)*D[w[x,y,z],y]+(b3*y*z+b2*y^2+b1*x^2+b0)*D[w[x,y,z],z]==(c3*z+c2*y+c1*x+c0)*w[x,y,z]+s1*x*y+s2*x*z;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1932*)


pde =  a*x*D[w[x,y,z],x]+b*x*D[w[x,y,z],y]+c*z*D[w[x,y,z],z]==k*x*w[x,y,z]+s*x^2;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1933*)


pde =  a*x*D[w[x,y,z],x]+b*y*D[w[x,y,z],y]+c*z*D[w[x,y,z],z]==k*x*w[x,y,z]+s*x^2;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1934*)


pde =  a*x^2*D[w[x,y,z],x]+b*y^2*D[w[x,y,z],y]+c*z^2*D[w[x,y,z],z]==(k*x+s)*w[x,y,z]+p*x+q;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1935*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+(a1*Sqrt[x]+a0)*D[w[x,y,z],y]+(b1*Sqrt[x]+b0)*D[w[x,y,z],z]==c*w[x,y,z]+ s1*Sqrt[x]+s0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1936*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+(b1*x^2+b0)*D[w[x,y,z],y]+(c1*y^3+c0)*D[w[x,y,z],z]==a*w[x,y,z]+ s1*x^3+s0;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1937*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+(a*y+k*x^3)*D[w[x,y,z],y]+(b*z+n*x^3)*D[w[x,y,z],z]==c*w[x,y,z]+ s*x^2;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1938*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+(a1*x*y+a2*x^3)*D[w[x,y,z],y]+(b1*y*z+b2*y^3)*D[w[x,y,z],z]==(c1*z+c2*y)*w[x,y,z]+ s1*x^2*y+s2*x*z^2;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1939*)


pde =  a*D[w[x,y,z],x]+b*y^3*D[w[x,y,z],y]+c*z^3*D[w[x,y,z],z]==x*w[x,y,z]+ k*x+s;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1940*)


pde =  a*D[w[x,y,z],x]+b*D[w[x,y,z],y]+c*D[w[x,y,z],z]==k*x^n*w[x,y,z]+ s*x^m;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1941*)


pde =  a*D[w[x,y,z],x]+b*y*D[w[x,y,z],y]+c*z*D[w[x,y,z],z]==k*x^n*w[x,y,z]+ s*x^m;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1942*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+a*z*D[w[x,y,z],y]+b*y*D[w[x,y,z],z]==c*x^n*w[x,y,z]+ s*x^m;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1943*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+a*x^n*D[w[x,y,z],y]+b*x^m*D[w[x,y,z],z]==c*x^k*w[x,y,z]+ s*x^r;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1944*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+b*x^n*D[w[x,y,z],y]+c*x^m*D[w[x,y,z],z]==a*w[x,y,z]+ s*x^k;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1945*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+(a*y+beta*x^n)*D[w[x,y,z],y]+(b*z+gamma*x^m)*D[w[x,y,z],z]==c*x^k*w[x,y,z]+ s*x^r;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1946*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+(a1*x^n1*y + a2*x^n2)*D[w[x,y,z],y]+(b1*y^m1*z + b2*y^m2)*D[w[x,y,z],z]==c*w[x,y,z]+ s1*x*y^k1+ s2*x^k2*z;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1947*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+(a1*x^lambda1*y + a2*x^lambda2*y^k)*D[w[x,y,z],y]+(b1*x^beta1*z + b2*x^beta2*z^m)*D[w[x,y,z],z]==c1*x^gamma1*w[x,y,z]+ c2*y^gamma2;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1948*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+(a1*x^lambda1*y + a2*x^lambda2*y^k)*D[w[x,y,z],y]+(b1*y^beta1*z + b2*y^beta2*z^m)*D[w[x,y,z],z]==c1*x^gamma1*w[x,y,z]+ c2*z^gamma2;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1949*)


pde =  x*D[w[x,y,z],x]+a*y*D[w[x,y,z],y]+b*z*D[w[x,y,z],z]==c*x^n*w[x,y,z]+ k*x^m;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1950*)


pde =  x*D[w[x,y,z],x]+a*z*D[w[x,y,z],y]+b*y*D[w[x,y,z],z]==c*x^n*w[x,y,z]+ k*x^m;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1951*)


pde =  b*c*x*D[w[x,y,z],x]+ c*(b*y + c*z)*D[w[x,y,z],y]+b*(b*y - c*z)*D[w[x,y,z],z]==k*x^n*w[x,y,z]+ s*x^m;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1952*)


pde =  b1*x^n1*D[w[x,y,z],x]+ b2*y^n2*D[w[x,y,z],y]+b3*z^n3*D[w[x,y,z],z]==a*w[x,y,z]+ c1*x^k1+c2*y^k2+c3*x^k3;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1953*)


pde =  a1*x^n1*D[w[x,y,z],x]+ a2*y^n2*D[w[x,y,z],y]+a3*z^n3*D[w[x,y,z],z]==b*x^k*w[x,y,z]+ c*x^m;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1954*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+ a*D[w[x,y,z],y]+b*D[w[x,y,z],z]==c*Exp[beta*x]*w[x,y,z]+ k*Exp[lambda*x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1955*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+ a*Exp[beta*x]*D[w[x,y,z],y]+b*Exp[lambda*x]*D[w[x,y,z],z]==c*Exp[gamma*x]*w[x,y,z]+ s*Exp[mu*x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1956*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+ b*Exp[beta*x]*D[w[x,y,z],y]+c*Exp[lambda*y]*D[w[x,y,z],z]==a*w[x,y,z]+ s*Exp[gamma*x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1957*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+ a*Exp[beta*x]*D[w[x,y,z],y]+b*Exp[lambda*z]*D[w[x,y,z],z]==c*w[x,y,z]+ k*Exp[gamma*x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1958*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+ (a1*Exp[sigma*x]+ a2*Exp[lambda*y] )*D[w[x,y,z],y]+(b1*Exp[mu*y]+ b2*Exp[beta*z])*D[w[x,y,z],z]==c1*w[x,y,z]+ c2*Exp[nu*x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1959*)


pde =  b1*Exp[lambda1*x]*D[w[x,y,z],x]+ b2*Exp[lambda2*y]*D[w[x,y,z],y]+b3*Exp[lambda3*z]*D[w[x,y,z],z]==a*w[x,y,z]+ c1*Exp[beta1*x]+c2*Exp[beta2*y]+c3*Exp[beta3*z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1960*)


pde =  a1*Exp[sigma1*x+beta1*y]*D[w[x,y,z],x]+ a2*Exp[sigma2*y+beta2*y]*D[w[x,y,z],y]+( b1*Exp[nu1*x+mu1*y] +  b2*Exp[nu2*x+mu2*y+lambda*z])*D[w[x,y,z],z]==c1*w[x,y,z]+ c2;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1961*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+ a*D[w[x,y,z],y]+b*D[w[x,y,z],z]==c*Exp[beta*x]*w[x,y,z]+ k*x^n;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1962*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+ a*x^n*D[w[x,y,z],y]+b*Exp[lambda*x]*D[w[x,y,z],z]==c*Exp[gamma*x]*w[x,y,z]+ s*x^k;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1963*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+ b*Exp[beta*x]*D[w[x,y,z],y]+c*y^n*D[w[x,y,z],z]==a*w[x,y,z]+ s*Exp[gamma*x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1964*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+ (a1*y+a2*x*y^k)*D[w[x,y,z],y]+(b1*x+b2*Exp[beta*y+lambda*z])*D[w[x,y,z],z]==c1*w[x,y,z]+ c2*Exp[gamma*x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1965*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+ (a1*x+a2*Exp[lambda*y])*D[w[x,y,z],y]+(b1*z+b2*Exp[beta*y]*z^k)*D[w[x,y,z],z]==c1*w[x,y,z]+ c2;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1966*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+ (a1*Exp[mu*x]+a2*Exp[lambda*y])*D[w[x,y,z],y]+(b1*Exp[nu*y]+b2*Exp[beta*z])*D[w[x,y,z],z]==c1*w[x,y,z]+ c2;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1967*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+ (a1*Exp[lambda1*x]*y+a2*Exp[lambda2*x])*D[w[x,y,z],y]+(b1*Exp[beta1*x]*z+b2*Exp[beta2*x])*D[w[x,y,z],z]==c1*Exp[gamma1*x]*w[x,y,z]+ c2*Exp[gamma2*x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1968*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+ (a1*Exp[lambda1*x]*y+a2*Exp[lambda2*x]*y^k)*D[w[x,y,z],y]+(b1*Exp[beta1*x]*z+b2*Exp[beta2*x]*z^m)*D[w[x,y,z],z]==c1*Exp[gamma1*x]*w[x,y,z]+ c2*Exp[gamma2*y];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1969*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+ (a1*Exp[lambda1*x]*y+a2*Exp[lambda2*x]*y^k)*D[w[x,y,z],y]+(b1*Exp[beta1*y]*z+b2*Exp[beta2*y]*z^m)*D[w[x,y,z],z]==c1*Exp[gamma1*x]*w[x,y,z]+ c2*Exp[gamma2*z];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1970*)


pde =  a1*Exp[beta*y]*D[w[x,y,z],x]+ a2*Exp[sigma*x]*D[w[x,y,z],y]+(b1*x^n*Exp[mu*y]+b2*Exp[nu*x+lambda*z])*D[w[x,y,z],z]==c1*w[x,y,z]+ c2;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1971*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+ a*D[w[x,y,z],y]+b*D[w[x,y,z],z]==c*Sinh[beta*x]^n*w[x,y,z]+ k*Sinh[lambda*x]^m;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1972*)


pde =  a*D[w[x,y,z],x]+ b*D[w[x,y,z],y]+c*Sinh[beta*z]*D[w[x,y,z],z]==(p*Sinh[lambda*x]+q)*w[x,y,z]+ k*Sinh[gamma*x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1973*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+ a*Sinh[beta*x]^n*D[w[x,y,z],y]+b*Sinh[lambda*x]^k*D[w[x,y,z],z]==c*w[x,y,z]+ k*Sinh[mu*x]^m;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1974*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+ b*Sinh[beta*x]^n*D[w[x,y,z],y]+c*Sinh[lambda*y]^k*D[w[x,y,z],z]==a*w[x,y,z]+ s*Sinh[mu*x]^m;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1975*)


pde =  b1*Sinh[lambda1*x]^n1*D[w[x,y,z],x]+ b2*Sinh[lambda2*x]^n2*D[w[x,y,z],y]+b3*Sinh[lambda3*x]^n3*D[w[x,y,z],z]==a*w[x,y,z]+ c1*Sinh[beta1*x]^k1+ c2*Sinh[beta2*x]^k2+ c3*Sinh[beta3*x]^k3;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1976*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+ a*D[w[x,y,z],y]+b*D[w[x,y,z],z]==c*Cosh[beta*x]^n*w[x,y,z]+ k*Cosh[lambda*x]^m;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1977*)


pde =  a*D[w[x,y,z],x]+ b*D[w[x,y,z],y]+c*Cosh[beta*z]*D[w[x,y,z],z]==(p*Cosh[lambda*x]+q)*w[x,y,z]+ k*Cosh[gamma*x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1978*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+ a*Cosh[beta*x]^n*D[w[x,y,z],y]+b*Cosh[lambda*x]^k*D[w[x,y,z],z]==c*w[x,y,z]+ k*Cosh[mu*x]^m;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1979*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+ b*Cosh[beta*x]^n*D[w[x,y,z],y]+c*Cosh[lambda*y]^k*D[w[x,y,z],z]==a*w[x,y,z]+ s*Cosh[mu*x]^m;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1980*)


pde =  b1*Cosh[lambda1*x]^n1*D[w[x,y,z],x]+ b2*Cosh[lambda2*x]^n2*D[w[x,y,z],y]+b3*Cosh[lambda3*x]^n3*D[w[x,y,z],z]==a*w[x,y,z]+ c1*Cosh[beta1*x]^k1+ c2*Cosh[beta2*x]^k2+ c3*Cosh[beta3*x]^k3;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1981*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+ a*D[w[x,y,z],y]+b*D[w[x,y,z],z]==c*Tanh[beta*x]^n*w[x,y,z]+ k*Tanh[lambda*x]^m;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1982*)


pde =  a*D[w[x,y,z],x]+ b*D[w[x,y,z],y]+c*Tanh[beta*z]*D[w[x,y,z],z]==(p*Tanh[lambda*x]+q)*w[x,y,z]+ k*Tanh[gamma*x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1983*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+ a*Tanh[beta*x]^n*D[w[x,y,z],y]+b*Tanh[lambda*x]^k*D[w[x,y,z],z]==c*w[x,y,z]+ k*Tanh[mu*x]^m;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1984*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+ b*Tanh[beta*x]^n*D[w[x,y,z],y]+c*Tanh[lambda*y]^k*D[w[x,y,z],z]==a*w[x,y,z]+ s*Tanh[mu*x]^m;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1985*)


pde =  b1*Tanh[lambda1*x]^n1*D[w[x,y,z],x]+ b2*Tanh[lambda2*x]^n2*D[w[x,y,z],y]+b3*Tanh[lambda3*x]^n3*D[w[x,y,z],z]==a*w[x,y,z]+ c1*Tanh[beta1*x]^k1+ c2*Tanh[beta2*x]^k2+ c3*Tanh[beta3*x]^k3;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1986*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+ a*D[w[x,y,z],y]+b*D[w[x,y,z],z]==c*Coth[beta*x]^n*w[x,y,z]+ k*Coth[lambda*x]^m;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1987*)


pde =  a*D[w[x,y,z],x]+ b*D[w[x,y,z],y]+c*Coth[beta*z]*D[w[x,y,z],z]==(p*Coth[lambda*x]+q)*w[x,y,z]+ k*Coth[gamma*x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1988*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+ a*Coth[beta*x]^n*D[w[x,y,z],y]+b*Coth[lambda*x]^k*D[w[x,y,z],z]==c*w[x,y,z]+ k*Coth[mu*x]^m;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1989*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+ b*Coth[beta*x]^n*D[w[x,y,z],y]+c*Coth[lambda*y]^k*D[w[x,y,z],z]==a*w[x,y,z]+ s*Coth[mu*x]^m;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1990*)


pde =  b1*Coth[lambda1*x]^n1*D[w[x,y,z],x]+ b2*Coth[lambda2*x]^n2*D[w[x,y,z],y]+b3*Coth[lambda3*x]^n3*D[w[x,y,z],z]==a*w[x,y,z]+ c1*Coth[beta1*x]^k1+ c2*Coth[beta2*x]^k2+ c3*Coth[beta3*x]^k3;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1991*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+ a*D[w[x,y,z],y]+b*D[w[x,y,z],z]==c*Sinh[beta*x]^n*w[x,y,z]+ k*Cosh[lambda*x]^m;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1992*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+ a*D[w[x,y,z],y]+b*D[w[x,y,z],z]==c*Tanh[beta*x]^n*w[x,y,z]+ k*Coth[lambda*x]^m;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1993*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+ b*Cosh[beta*x]^n*D[w[x,y,z],y]+c*Sinh[lambda*y]^k*D[w[x,y,z],z]==a*w[x,y,z]+ s*Cosh[mu*x]^m;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1994*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+ a*Tanh[beta*x]^n*D[w[x,y,z],y]+b*Coth[lambda*x]^k*D[w[x,y,z],z]==c*w[x,y,z]+ s*Tanh[mu*x]^m;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1995*)


pde =  b1*Sinh[lambda1*x]^n1*D[w[x,y,z],x]+ b2*Cosh[lambda2*x]^n2*D[w[x,y,z],y]+b3*Sinh[lambda3*x]^n3*D[w[x,y,z],z]==a*w[x,y,z]+ c1*Cosh[beta1*x]^k1+ c2*Sinh[beta2*x]^k2+ c3*Sinh[beta3*x]^k3;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1996*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+ a*D[w[x,y,z],y]+b*D[w[x,y,z],z]==c*Log[beta*x]^n*w[x,y,z]+ k*Log[lambda*x]^m;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1997*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+ a*Log[beta*x]^n*D[w[x,y,z],y]+b*Log[lambda*x]^k*D[w[x,y,z],z]==c*w[x,y,z]+ s*Log[mu*x]^m;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1998*)


pde =  D[w[x,y,z],x]+ b*Log[beta*x]^n*D[w[x,y,z],y]+c*Log[lambda*y]^k*D[w[x,y,z],z]==a*w[x,y,z]+ s*Log[mu*x]^m;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 1999*)


pde =  a*Log[alpha*x]*D[w[x,y,z],x]+ b*Log[beta*y]*D[w[x,y,z],y]+c*Log[gamma*z]*D[w[x,y,z],z]==p*w[x,y,z]+ q*Log[lambda*x];
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 2000*)


pde =  b1*Log[lambda1*x]^n1*D[w[x,y,z],x]+ b2*Log[lambda2*x]^n2*D[w[x,y,z],y]+b3*Log[lambda3*x]^n3*D[w[x,y,z],z]==a*w[x,y,z]+ c1*Log[beta1*x]^k1+ c2*Log[beta2*x]^k2+ c3*Log[beta3*x]^k3;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, y,z], {x,y,z}], 60*10]];
 
(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 2001*)

pde =  D[w[x, t], t] == a*D[w[x, t], {x, 2}] + b*w[x, t]^2;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 2002*)


pde =  D[w[x, t], t] == D[w[x, t], {x, 2}] + a*w[x, t]*(1 - w[x, t]);
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 2003*)


pde =  D[w[x, t], t] == a*D[w[x, t], {x, 2}] - b*w[x, t]^3;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 2004*)


pde =  D[w[x, t], t] == D[w[x, t], {x, 2}] + a*w[x, t] - b*w[x, t]^3;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 2005*)


pde =  D[w[x, t], t] == a*D[w[x, t], {x, 2}] - b*w[x, t]^3 - c*w[x, t]^2;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 2006*)


pde =  D[w[x, t], t] == D[w[x, t], {x, 2}] - w[x, t]*(1 - w[x, t])*(a - w[x, t]);
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 2007*)

pde = D[w[x, t], t] == a*D[w[x, t], {x, 2}] + b0 + b1*w[x, t] + b2*w[x, t]^2 + b3*w[x, t]^3;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 2008*)


pde =  D[w[x, t], t] == a*D[w[x, t], {x, 2}] + b*w[x, t]^k;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 2009*)


pde =  D[w[x, t], t] == D[w[x, t], {x, 2}] + a*w[x, t] + b*w[x, t]^m;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 2010*)


pde =  D[w[x, t], t] == D[w[x, t], {x, 2}] + a*w[x, t] + b*w[x, t]^m + c*w[x, t]^(2*m - 1);
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 2011*)

 
pde =  D[w[x, t], t] == D[w[x, t], {x, 2}] + a*w[x, t]^(m - 1) + b*m*w[x, t]^m - m*b^2*w[x, t]^(2*m - 1);
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];

(*----------------------------*)
(*PDE number 2012*)

pde = D[w[x, t], t] == a*D[w[x, t], {x, 2}] + s1*(b*x + c*t)^k + s2*w[x, t]^m;
sol =  AbsoluteTiming[TimeConstrained[DSolve[pde, w[x, t], {x, t}], 60*10]];
